     4→
     5→Incremental evolution from Dual v2.0.0 to v3.0.0. v2.x releases fix bugs, complete unimplemented features, and improve CLI ergonomics — all within the current architecture. v3.0.0 is the architectural rewrite (multiplexer trait, TUI, plugin system). This plan covers Linear project cleanup, every v2.x change, and v3 scoping.

   125→## Phase 1: v2.1.0 — Bug Fixes & Completions
   126→
   127→### Overview
   128→Fix the three fundamental bugs and complete unimplemented features. All changes are within existing modules — no new modules, no new dependencies.
   129→
   130→### Changes Required:
   131→
   132→#### 1. Fix Branch Model (Bug 1)
   133→**Files**: `src/clone.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   134→
   135→**Current behavior**: `git clone -b <branch>` fails when branch doesn't exist at origin.
   136→
   137→**New behavior**: Clone from local main workspace using `--local`, then `git checkout -b <new-branch>`.
   138→
   139→**Implementation**:
   140→
   141→In `src/clone.rs`, change `clone_workspace()`:
   142→```rust
   143→// Instead of:
   144→//   git clone -b <branch> <url> <target_dir>
   145→// Do:
   146→//   1. Find local main workspace path for this repo
   147→//   2. git clone --local <main_workspace_path> <target_dir>
   148→//   3. cd <target_dir> && git checkout -b <branch>
   149→```
   150→
   151→In `src/main.rs`, update `cmd_create()`:
   152→- Look up the main workspace (the one with `path: Some(...)`) for the given repo
   153→- Pass its path to a new `clone_from_local()` function
   154→- If no main workspace exists, fall back to `git clone <url>` then `git checkout -b`
   155→
   156→New function signature in `clone.rs`:
   157→```rust
   158→pub fn clone_from_local(
   159→    main_workspace_path: &Path,
   160→    target_dir: &Path,
   161→    new_branch: &str,
   162→) -> Result<(), CloneError>
   163→```
   164→
   165→**Tests to add**:
   166→- `test_clone_from_local_args` — verify git clone --local + git checkout -b args
   167→- `test_clone_fallback_to_remote` — verify fallback when no local main exists
   168→- Update existing `test_clone_workspace_args` — adjust for new behavior
   169→
   170→#### 2. Fix Default `.dual.toml` (Bug 3)
   171→**File**: `src/config.rs`
   172→
   173→**Current behavior**: `toml::to_string_pretty()` generates bare TOML without comments.
   174→
   175→**New behavior**: Hand-write a commented template string instead of serializing.
   176→
   177→**Implementation**:
   178→
   179→Replace `write_hints()` to write a hand-crafted template:
   180→```rust
   181→pub fn write_default_hints(repo_root: &Path) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {
   182→    let template = r#"# Dual workspace configuration
   183→# See: https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual
   184→
   185→# Docker image for the container runtime
   186→image = "node:20"
   187→
   188→# Ports your dev server uses (for reverse proxy routing)
   189→# Example: ports = [3000, 3001]
   190→# ports = []
   191→
   192→# Shell command to run after container creation (e.g., dependency install)
   193→# Example: setup = "pnpm install"
   194→# setup = ""
   195→
   196→# Environment variables passed to the container
   197→# Example:
   198→# [env]
   199→# NODE_ENV = "development"
   200→
   201→# Commands to route to the container (in addition to defaults)
   202→# Default: npm, npx, pnpm, node, python, python3, pip, pip3, curl, make
   203→# Example: extra_commands = ["cargo", "go", "ruby"]
   204→# extra_commands = []
   205→
   206→# Directories to isolate with anonymous Docker volumes
   207→# These directories get their own volume so they don't sync between host/container
   208→# Example: anonymous_volumes = ["node_modules", ".next", "target"]
   209→# anonymous_volumes = ["node_modules"]
   210→
   211→# Files to share across all workspaces of this repo
   212→# These are gitignored files that should be available in every branch workspace
   213→# [shared]
   214→# files = [".env.local", ".vercel"]
   215→"#;
   216→    let hints_path = repo_root.join(".dual.toml");
   217→    std::fs::write(&hints_path, template)?;
   218→    Ok(())
   219→}
   220→```
   221→
   222→Update `cmd_add()` in `src/main.rs:101-111` to call `write_default_hints()` instead of `write_hints(&RepoHints::default())`.
   223→
   224→Keep `write_hints()` for programmatic updates — it serializes actual `RepoHints` structs.
   225→
   226→#### 3. Wire `env` Vars to Container Creation
   227→**Files**: `src/container.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   228→
   229→**Current behavior**: `env` HashMap loaded at `main.rs:262` then dropped. `container::create()` takes only `(name, dir, image)`.
   230→
   231→**Implementation**:
   232→
   233→Update `container::create()` signature:
   234→```rust
   235→pub fn create(
   236→    name: &str,
   237→    workspace_dir: &Path,
   238→    image: &str,
   239→    env: &HashMap<String, String>,
   240→    anonymous_volumes: &[String],
   241→) -> Result<(), ContainerError>
   242→```
   243→
   244→In `build_create_args()`, add env vars:
   245→```rust
   246→for (key, value) in env {
   247→    args.push("-e".to_string());
   248→    args.push(format!("{key}={value}"));
   249→}
   250→```
   251→
   252→Replace hardcoded `node_modules` volume with configurable `anonymous_volumes`:
   253→```rust
   254→for vol in anonymous_volumes {
   255→    args.push("-v".to_string());
   256→    args.push(format!("/workspace/{vol}"));
   257→}
   258→```
   259→
   260→Update `cmd_launch()` in `main.rs` to pass `hints.env` and `hints.anonymous_volumes` to `container::create()`.
   261→
   262→#### 4. Wire `setup` Command Execution
   263→**Files**: `src/container.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   264→
   265→**Current behavior**: `setup` field exists in `RepoHints` but is never executed.
   266→
   267→**Implementation**:
   268→
   269→Add to `container.rs`:
   270→```rust
   271→pub fn exec_setup(name: &str, setup_cmd: &str) -> Result<(), ContainerError> {
   272→    // docker exec <name> sh -c "<setup_cmd>"
   273→}
   274→```
   275→
   276→In `cmd_launch()`, after `container::start()` and before tmux session creation:
   277→```rust
   278→if let Some(setup) = &hints.setup {
   279→    info!("Running setup: {setup}");
   280→    container::exec_setup(&container_name, setup)?;
   281→}
   282→```
   283→
   284→#### 5. Configurable Container Commands
   285→**Files**: `src/config.rs`, `src/shell.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   286→
   287→**Current behavior**: `CONTAINER_COMMANDS` at `shell.rs:2-4` is a hardcoded `&[&str]`.
   288→
   289→**Implementation**:
   290→
   291→Add to `RepoHints` in `config.rs`:
   292→```rust
   293→#[serde(default)]
   294→pub extra_commands: Vec<String>,
   295→
   296→#[serde(default)]
   297→pub anonymous_volumes: Vec<String>,
   298→```
   299→
   300→Update `Default` impl:
   301→```rust
   302→extra_commands: Vec::new(),
   303→anonymous_volumes: vec!["node_modules".to_string()],
   304→```
   305→
   306→Update `shell.rs` to accept additional commands:
   307→```rust
   308→pub fn write_rc_file(
   309→    container_name: &str,
   310→    extra_commands: &[String],
   311→) -> Result<PathBuf, ShellError>
   312→```
   313→
   314→Merge `CONTAINER_COMMANDS` with `extra_commands` when generating RC.
   315→
   316→#### 6. Update `cmd_launch()` Pipeline
   317→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   318→
   319→Wire all the new parameters through the launch pipeline:
   320→```
   321→load hints → pass hints.env + hints.anonymous_volumes to container::create()
   322→           → pass hints.setup to container::exec_setup()
   323→           → pass hints.extra_commands to shell::write_rc_file()
   324→```
   325→

   350→## Phase 2: v2.2.0 — Context Awareness & UX
   351→
   352→### Overview
   353→Make the CLI context-aware so commands auto-detect the current repo/workspace from the working directory. Fix tmux nested session handling.
   354→
   355→### Changes Required:
   356→
   357→#### 1. Context-Aware `dual create`
   358→**Files**: `src/cli.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   359→
   360→Make `repo` optional in `Create` command:
   361→```rust
   362→Create {
   363→    /// Branch name
   364→    branch: String,
   365→    /// Repo name (auto-detected if omitted)
   366→    #[arg(long)]
   367→    repo: Option<String>,
   368→},
   369→```
   370→
   371→In `cmd_create()`, if `repo` is `None`:
   372→1. Call `detect_git_repo()` to get the current repo info
   373→2. Look up the repo name from state by matching the git remote URL
   374→3. If found, use it. If not, error with a helpful message.
   375→
   376→This means: `dual create feat/auth` works when inside a repo directory.
   377→
   378→#### 2. Context-Aware `launch`, `destroy`, `open`, `urls`
   379→**Files**: `src/cli.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   380→
   381→Make the workspace arg optional for these commands:
   382→```rust
   383→Launch {
   384→    /// Workspace identifier (auto-detected if omitted)
   385→    workspace: Option<String>,
   386→},
   387→Destroy {
   388→    workspace: Option<String>,
   389→},
   390→```
   391→
   392→When workspace is `None`, call `detect_workspace()` to find the current workspace.
   393→
   394→#### 3. Tmux Nested Session Detection
   395→**File**: `src/tmux.rs`
   396→
   397→Add detection in `attach()`:
   398→```rust
   399→pub fn attach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
   400→    if std::env::var("TMUX").is_ok() {
   401→        // Already in tmux — use switch-client instead of attach
   402→        // tmux switch-client -t <session_name>
   403→    } else {
   404→        // Not in tmux — use attach-session
   405→        // tmux attach-session -t <session_name>
   406→    }
   407→}
   408→```
   409→
   410→#### 4. Improve `dual list` Output
   411→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   412→
   413→Group workspaces by repo in the list output:
   414→```
   415→lightfast
   416→  main          ● running    (container: up, tmux: attached)
   417→  feat/auth     ○ stopped    (container: stopped, tmux: none)
   418→  feat/billing  ◌ lazy       (not cloned yet)
   419→
   420→agent-os
   421→  main          ● running    (container: up, tmux: background)
   422→```
   423→

   443→
   444→## Phase 3: v3.0.0 — Architecture Rewrite (Scoping Only)
   445→
   446→### Overview
   447→This phase is scoped but NOT detailed for implementation yet. It defines what v3 contains so the v2.x work doesn't accidentally paint us into a corner.
   448→
   449→### 3.1 Multiplexer Trait
   450→
   451→Extract `src/tmux.rs` functions into a trait:
   452→```rust
   453→pub trait MultiplexerBackend {
   454→    fn create_session(&self, name: &str, cwd: &Path, init_cmd: Option<&str>) -> Result<()>;
   455→    fn attach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   456→    fn detach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   457→    fn destroy(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   458→    fn is_alive(&self, name: &str) -> bool;
   459→    fn list_sessions(&self) -> Vec<String>;
   460→    fn send_keys(&self, name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<()>;
   461→    fn session_name(&self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String;
   462→}
   463→```
   464→
   465→Implement `TmuxBackend`, stub `ZellijBackend`, add `BasicBackend` (no multiplexer).
   466→
   467→### 3.2 TUI
   468→
   469→Add ratatui + crossterm dependencies. New `src/tui/` module:
   470→- `mod.rs` — TUI app state and event loop
   471→- `tree.rs` — repo/branch tree view
   472→- `status.rs` — workspace status panel
   473→
   474→`dual` (no args) launches TUI. `dual list` becomes the non-interactive fallback.
   475→
   476→### 3.3 Session Layout Configuration
   477→
   478→Add to `.dual.toml`:
   479→```toml
   480→[layout]
   481→type = "editor-claude-shell"  # or custom
   482→
   483→[[layout.panes]]
   484→command = "nvim ."
   485→size = "60%"
   486→
   487→[[layout.panes]]
   488→command = "claude"
   489→size = "20%"
   490→
   491→[[layout.panes]]
   492→command = ""  # shell
   493→size = "20%"
   494→```
   495→
   496→### 3.4 Plugin System (Future)
   497→
   498→Design TBD. Likely TOML-declared hooks + shell commands:
   499→```toml
   500→[hooks]
   501→post_launch = "pnpm install && pnpm dev"
   502→pre_destroy = "pnpm build"
   503→```
   504→
   505→### What v3 Requires That v2.x Must NOT Break:
   506→- `config.rs` naming conventions must remain stable
   507→- `WorkspaceEntry` struct can add fields but not change existing ones
   508→- `container.rs` function signatures can evolve (already changing in v2.1)
   509→- `.dual.toml` format must remain backward-compatible (new fields with defaults)
   510→

   539→| Multiplexer trait: extract tmux behind backend interface | High | Core, Tmux | Create `MultiplexerBackend` trait, implement `TmuxBackend` |
   540→| TUI: ratatui-based workspace browser | High | Core, DX | Add ratatui/crossterm deps, implement tree view, `dual` → TUI |
   541→| Session layout configuration | Medium | Core, Tmux | Add `[layout]` section to `.dual.toml` for pane/window config |
   542→| ZellijBackend stub | Medium | Core | Implement `MultiplexerBackend` for zellij |
   543→| BasicBackend: no-multiplexer fallback | Low | Core | Implement `MultiplexerBackend` that just runs in current terminal |
   544→
   545→---
   546→
   547→## Testing Strategy
   548→
   549→### Unit Tests:
   550→- Clone module: test local clone args, fallback behavior, checkout -b construction
   551→- Config module: test template generation, extra_commands/anonymous_volumes parsing
   552→- Container module: test env var args, anonymous volume args, setup exec args
   553→- Shell module: test merged command lists (defaults + extras)
   554→- Tmux module: test `$TMUX` detection, switch-client vs attach args
   555→- CLI module: test optional args parsing
   556→
   557→### Integration Tests:
   558→- Full launch pipeline with env vars → verify env inside container
   559→- Full launch pipeline with setup command → verify setup ran
   560→- Clone from local → verify new branch exists
   561→- Context detection → verify auto-detect from cwd
   562→
   563→### Manual Testing Steps:
   564→1. Create a workspace with `dual create myrepo feat/new-thing` where `feat/new-thing` doesn't exist at origin
   565→2. Verify `.dual.toml` has helpful comments when created by `dual add`
   566→3. Add env vars to `.dual.toml`, launch, verify inside container
   567→4. Set `setup = "echo hello > /tmp/setup-ran"`, launch, verify file exists
   568→5. Run `dual create feat/test` from inside a repo directory (no repo arg)
   569→6. Run `dual launch` from inside tmux — verify no nesting
   570→
   571→## Performance Considerations
   572→
   573→- Clone from local with `--local` uses hardlinks — significantly faster than remote clone
   574→- `detect_workspace()` and `detect_git_repo()` run git commands — acceptable for interactive CLI, but TUI (v3) should cache results
   575→- Anonymous volumes list is typically small (1-3 items) — no performance concern
   576→
   577→## Migration Notes
   578→
   579→### v2.0.0 → v2.1.0
   580→- **`.dual.toml` backward compatible**: New fields (`extra_commands`, `anonymous_volumes`) have defaults via `#[serde(default)]`
   581→- **Existing workspaces**: Already-cloned workspaces are unaffected. Only new `dual create` uses the new clone strategy
   582→- **Existing containers**: Must be recreated to pick up env vars/anonymous volumes. `dual destroy` + `dual launch` handles this
   583→
   584→### v2.1.0 → v2.2.0
   585→- **CLI breaking change**: `dual create` positional args change from `<repo> <branch>` to `<branch> [--repo REPO]`. This is a minor breaking change — document in changelog
   586→- **Optional workspace args**: Previously required args become optional. Fully backward compatible (explicit args still work)
   587→
   588→### v2.2.0 → v3.0.0
   589→- **TUI as default**: `dual` (no args) changes from showing list to launching TUI. `dual list` remains as non-interactive fallback
   590→- **Multiplexer trait**: Internal refactor, no user-facing change unless using zellij
   591→
   592→## References
   593→
   594→- Research: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-v3-architecture-rethink.md`
   595→- Earlier research: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-architecture-rethink-bugs-tui-plugins.md`
   596→- TUI design: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-02-15-dual-tui-design.md`
   597→- SPEC.md: Backend trait contract (lines 269-294), TUI (lines 298-320, 397)
   598→- Linear project: https://linear.app/jps0000-dual/project/dual-v0-7f8871a38577
   599→

    69→/// Register the current repo as a dual workspace.
    70→fn cmd_add(name: Option<&str>) -> i32 {
    71→    // Detect git repo info from current directory

    86→    // Load or create state

    92→        }
    93→    };
    94→
    95→    // Check for duplicates
    96→    if st.has_workspace(&repo_name, &branch) {
    97→        error!("workspace {}/{} already exists", repo_name, branch);
    98→        return 1;
    99→    }
   100→
   101→    // Check for .dual.toml — if missing, create a default one
   102→    let hints_path = repo_root.join(".dual.toml");
   103→    if !hints_path.exists() {
   104→        let hints = config::RepoHints::default();
   105→        if let Err(e) = config::write_hints(&repo_root, &hints) {
   106→            warn!("failed to write .dual.toml: {e}");
   107→        } else {
   108→            info!("Created .dual.toml with defaults (image: node:20)");
   109→            info!("Edit it to customize ports, image, setup command, and env vars.");
   110→        }
   111→    }
   112→
   113→    // Initialize shared directory if [shared] is configured
   114→    let hints = config::load_hints(&repo_root).unwrap_or_default();
   115→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   116→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   117→    {
   118→        match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&repo_name) {
   119→            Ok(shared_dir) => {
   120→                match shared::init_from_main(&repo_root, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   121→                    Ok(moved) => {
   122→                        for f in &moved {
   123→                            info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{repo_name}/");
   124→                        }
   125→                    }
   126→                    Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   127→                }
   128→            }
   129→            Err(e) => warn!("could not create shared directory: {e}"),
   130→        }
   131→    }
   132→
   133→    // Add workspace entry
   134→    let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
   135→        repo: repo_name.clone(),
   136→        url,
   137→        branch: branch.clone(),
   138→        path: Some(repo_root.to_string_lossy().to_string()),
   139→    };
   140→
   141→    if let Err(e) = st.add_workspace(entry) {
   142→        error!("{e}");
   143→        return 1;
   144→    }
   145→
   146→    // Save state
   147→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   148→        error!("failed to save state: {e}");
   149→        return 1;
   150→    }
   151→
   152→    let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&repo_name, &branch);
   153→    info!("Added workspace: {ws_id}");
   154→    info!("Use `dual launch {ws_id}` to start.");
   155→    0
   156→}

   208→/// Launch a specific workspace: clone → container → shell RC → tmux → attach.
   209→fn cmd_launch(workspace: &str) -> i32 {
   210→    let st = match state::load() {
   211→        Ok(s) => s,
   212→        Err(e) => {
   213→            error!("{e}");
   214→            return 1;
   215→        }
   216→    };
   217→
   218→    let entry = match st.resolve_workspace(workspace) {
   219→        Some(e) => e,
   220→        None => {
   221→            error!("unknown workspace '{workspace}'");
   222→            info!("Configured workspaces:");
   223→            for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   224→                let id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   225→                info!("  {id}");
   226→            }
   227→            return 1;
   228→        }
   229→    };
   230→
   231→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   232→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   233→    let session_name = tmux::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   234→    debug!(
   235→        repo = %entry.repo,
   236→        branch = %entry.branch,
   237→        "resolved workspace"
   238→    );
   239→
   240→    // Step 1: Resolve workspace directory
   241→    let workspace_dir = if let Some(ref path) = entry.path {
   242→        let dir = PathBuf::from(path);
   243→        if !dir.join(".git").exists() {
   244→            error!(
   245→                "workspace path {} does not contain a git repo",
   246→                dir.display()
   247→            );
   248→            return 1;
   249→        }
   250→        dir
   251→    } else {
   252→        match clone::clone_workspace(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.url, &entry.branch) {
   253→            Ok(dir) => dir,
   254→            Err(e) => {
   255→                error!("clone failed: {e}");
   256→                return 1;
   257→            }
   258→        }
   259→    };
   260→
   261→    // Step 2: Handle shared files
   262→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   263→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   264→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   265→        && let Ok(shared_dir) = shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo)
   266→    {
   267→        if entry.path.is_some() {
   268→            // Main workspace: ensure shared files are initialized
   269→            match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   270→                Ok(moved) => {
   271→                    for f in &moved {
   272→                        info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{}/", entry.repo);
   273→                    }
   274→                }
   275→                Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   276→            }
   277→        } else {
   278→            // Branch workspace: copy shared files
   279→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   280→                Ok(copied) => {
   281→                    for f in &copied {
   282→                        info!("  shared: copied {f}");
   283→                    }
   284→                }
   285→                Err(e) => warn!("shared copy failed: {e}"),
   286→            }
   287→        }
   288→    }
   289→
   290→    // Step 3: Ensure container exists and is running
   291→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   292→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {
   293→            info!("Creating container {container_name}...");
   294→            if let Err(e) = container::create(&container_name, &workspace_dir, &hints.image) {
   295→                error!("container create failed: {e}");
   296→                return 1;
   297→            }
   298→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   299→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   300→                return 1;
   301→            }
   302→        }
   303→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   304→            info!("Starting container {container_name}...");
   305→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   306→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   307→                return 1;
   308→            }
   309→        }
   310→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {}
   311→    }
   312→
   313→    // Step 4: Write shell RC file
   314→    let rc_path = match shell::write_rc_file(&container_name) {
   315→        Ok(p) => p,
   316→        Err(e) => {
   317→            error!("failed to write shell RC: {e}");
   318→            return 1;
   319→        }
   320→    };
   321→
   322→    // Step 5: Create tmux session if not alive
   323→    if !tmux::is_alive(&session_name) {
   324→        let source_cmd = shell::source_file_command(&rc_path);
   325→        if let Err(e) = tmux::create_session(&session_name, &workspace_dir, Some(&source_cmd)) {
   326→            error!("tmux session creation failed: {e}");
   327→            return 1;
   328→        }
   329→    }
   330→
   331→    // Step 6: Attach
   332→    info!("Attaching to {session_name}...");
   333→    if let Err(e) = tmux::attach(&session_name) {
   334→        error!("tmux attach failed: {e}");
   335→        return 1;
   336→    }
   337→
   338→    0
   339→}

   361→/// Destroy a workspace: tmux → container → clone.
   362→fn cmd_destroy(workspace: &str) -> i32 {
   363→    let mut st = match state::load() {
   364→        Ok(s) => s,
   365→        Err(e) => {
   366→            error!("{e}");
   367→            return 1;
   368→        }
   369→    };
   370→
   371→    let entry = match st.resolve_workspace(workspace) {
   372→        Some(e) => e.clone(),
   373→        None => {
   374→            error!("unknown workspace '{workspace}'");
   375→            return 1;
   376→        }
   377→    };
   378→
   379→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   380→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   381→    let session_name = tmux::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   382→
   383→    // Destroy tmux session
   384→    if tmux::is_alive(&session_name) {
   385→        info!("Destroying tmux session {session_name}...");
   386→        if let Err(e) = tmux::destroy(&session_name) {
   387→            warn!("tmux destroy failed: {e}");
   388→        }
   389→    }
   390→
   391→    // Stop and remove container
   392→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   393→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {
   394→            info!("Stopping container {container_name}...");
   395→            if let Err(e) = container::stop(&container_name) {
   396→                warn!("container stop failed: {e}");
   397→            }
   398→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   399→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   400→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   401→            }
   402→        }
   403→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   404→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   405→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   406→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   407→            }
   408→        }
   409→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {}
   410→    }
   411→
   412→    // Remove clone (only for non-explicit-path workspaces)
   413→    if entry.path.is_none() && clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch)
   414→    {
   415→        info!("Removing clone...");
   416→        if let Err(e) = clone::remove_workspace(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch) {
   417→            error!("failed to remove clone: {e}");
   418→            return 1;
   419→        }
   420→    }
   421→
   422→    // Remove from state
   423→    st.remove_workspace(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   424→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   425→        warn!("failed to save state: {e}");
   426→    }
   427→
   428→    info!("Workspace '{workspace}' destroyed.");
   429→    0
   430→}

   432→/// Open workspace services in the default browser.
   433→fn cmd_open(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   434→    let st = match state::load() {
   435→        Ok(s) => s,
   436→        Err(e) => {
   437→            error!("{e}");
   438→            return 1;
   439→        }
   440→    };
   441→
   442→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   443→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   444→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   445→        return 0;
   446→    }
   447→
   448→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   449→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   450→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   451→        None => url_groups,
   452→    };
   453→
   454→    if filtered.is_empty() {
   455→        if let Some(ws) = &workspace {
   456→            error!("no URLs for workspace '{ws}'");
   457→        }
   458→        return 1;
   459→    }
   460→
   461→    for (_, urls) in &filtered {
   462→        for url_line in urls {
   463→            // Extract the URL part (after the status icon)
   464→            let url = url_line
   465→                .trim()
   466→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cf}')
   467→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cb}')
   468→                .trim();
   469→            let http_url = format!("http://{url}");
   470→            #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
   471→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("open").arg(&http_url).spawn();
   472→            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
   473→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("xdg-open")
   474→                .arg(&http_url)
   475→                .spawn();
   476→            info!("Opening {http_url}");
   477→        }
   478→    }
   479→
   480→    0
   481→}
   482→
   483→/// Show workspace URLs.
   484→fn cmd_urls(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   485→    let st = match state::load() {
   486→        Ok(s) => s,
   487→        Err(e) => {
   488→            error!("{e}");
   489→            return 1;
   490→        }
   491→    };
   492→
   493→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   494→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   495→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   496→        return 0;
   497→    }
   498→
   499→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   500→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   501→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   502→        None => url_groups,
   503→    };
   504→
   505→    for (workspace_id, urls) in &filtered {
   506→        info!("{workspace_id}");
   507→        for url in urls {

   516→/// Start the reverse proxy.
   517→fn cmd_proxy() -> i32 {
   518→    let st = match state::load() {
   519→        Ok(s) => s,
   520→        Err(e) => {
   521→            error!("{e}");
   522→            return 1;
   523→        }
   524→    };
   525→
   526→    let rt = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().expect("failed to create tokio runtime");
   527→    match rt.block_on(proxy::start(&st)) {
   528→        Ok(()) => 0,
   529→        Err(e) => {
   530→            error!("proxy failed: {e}");
   531→            1
   532→        }
   533→    }
   534→}
   535→
   536→/// Output shell RC for a container (used by `eval "$(dual shell-rc <name>)"`).
   537→fn cmd_shell_rc(container_name: &str) -> i32 {
   538→    print!("{}", shell::generate_rc(container_name));
   539→    0
   540→}
   541→
   542→/// Sync shared config files for a workspace.
   543→fn cmd_sync(workspace_arg: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   544→    let st = match state::load() {
   545→        Ok(s) => s,
   546→        Err(e) => {
   547→            error!("{e}");
   548→            return 1;
   549→        }
   550→    };
   551→
   552→    // Resolve which workspace we're syncing
   553→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   554→        match st.resolve_workspace(&ws) {
   555→            Some(e) => e.clone(),
   556→            None => {
   557→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   558→                return 1;
   559→            }
   560→        }
   561→    } else {
   562→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   563→            Some(e) => e,
   564→            None => {
   565→                error!("not inside a dual workspace");
   566→                info!("Usage: dual sync [workspace]");
   567→                return 1;
   568→            }
   569→        }
   570→    };
   571→
   572→    // Load hints
   573→    let workspace_dir = st.workspace_dir(&entry);
   574→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   575→    let shared_config = match &hints.shared {
   576→        Some(s) if !s.files.is_empty() => s,
   577→        _ => {
   578→            error!("no [shared] section in .dual.toml (or files list is empty)");
   579→            return 1;
   580→        }
   581→    };
   582→
   583→    let shared_dir = match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo) {
   584→        Ok(d) => d,
   585→        Err(e) => {
   586→            error!("{e}");
   587→            return 1;
   588→        }
   589→    };
   590→
   591→    let is_main = entry.path.is_some();
   592→
   593→    if is_main {
   594→        // Main workspace: init shared dir, then prompt to sync all branches
   595→        match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   596→            Ok(moved) => {
   597→                for f in &moved {
   598→                    info!("  moved {f} → shared/");
   599→                }
   600→            }
   601→            Err(e) => {
   602→                error!("{e}");
   603→                return 1;
   604→            }
   605→        }
   606→
   607→        // Prompt to sync all branches
   608→        let branches: Vec<_> = st
   609→            .workspaces_for_repo(&entry.repo)
   610→            .into_iter()
   611→            .filter(|ws| ws.path.is_none())
   612→            .collect();
   613→
   614→        if branches.is_empty() {
   615→            info!("No branch workspaces to sync.");
   616→            return 0;
   617→        }
   618→
   619→        // Interactive prompt — use println! directly since this is user interaction
   620→        println!(
   621→            "\nSync shared files to ALL {} branch workspace(s)? [y/N]",
   622→            branches.len()
   623→        );
   624→        let mut input = String::new();
   625→        std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut input).unwrap_or(0);
   626→        if !input.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("y") {
   627→            info!("Cancelled.");
   628→            return 0;
   629→        }
   630→
   631→        for branch_entry in &branches {
   632→            let branch_dir = st.workspace_dir(branch_entry);
   633→            if !branch_dir.exists() {
   634→                continue; // Not yet cloned
   635→            }
   636→            let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&branch_entry.repo, &branch_entry.branch);
   637→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&branch_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   638→                Ok(copied) => {
   639→                    info!("{ws_id}: synced {} file(s)", copied.len());
   640→                }
   641→                Err(e) => error!("{ws_id}: {e}"),
   642→            }
   643→        }
   644→    } else {
   645→        // Branch workspace: copy from shared dir
   646→        match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   647→            Ok(copied) => {
   648→                if copied.is_empty() {
   649→                    info!(
   650→                        "No shared files available yet. Run `dual sync` in the main workspace first."
   651→                    );
   652→                } else {
   653→                    for f in &copied {
   654→                        info!("  synced {f}");
   655→                    }
   656→                }
   657→            }
   658→            Err(e) => {
   659→                error!("{e}");
   660→                return 1;
   661→            }
   662→        }
   663→    }
   664→
   665→    0
   666→}
   667→
   668→/// Detect which workspace the current directory belongs to.
   669→fn detect_workspace(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<state::WorkspaceEntry> {
   670→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
   671→
   672→    // Try git root first (handles being in subdirectories)
   673→    let root = std::process::Command::new("git")
   674→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   675→        .output()
   676→        .ok()
   677→        .filter(|o| o.status.success())
   678→        .map(|o| PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()))
   679→        .unwrap_or(cwd);
   680→
   681→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   682→        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);
   683→        if ws_dir == root {
   684→            return Some(ws.clone());
   685→        }
   686→    }
   687→    None
   688→}
   689→
   690→/// Print workspace status table.
   691→fn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState) {
   692→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   693→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   694→        let workspace_id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   695→        let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   696→        let session_name = tmux::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   697→
   698→        let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {
   699→            // Explicit path — check if the path exists
   700→            ws.path
   701→                .as_ref()
   702→                .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(".git").exists())
   703→                .unwrap_or(false)
   704→        } else {
   705→            clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)
   706→        };
   707→        let container_st = container::status(&container_name);
   708→        let tmux_alive = tmux::is_alive(&session_name);
   709→
   710→        let status_icon = match (&container_st, tmux_alive) {
   711→            (container::ContainerStatus::Running, true) => "\u{25cf} attached",
   712→            (container::ContainerStatus::Running, false) => "\u{25cf} running",
   713→            (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, _) => "\u{25cb} stopped",
   714→            (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) if clone_exists => "\u{25cb} stopped",
   715→            (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) => "\u{25cc} lazy",
   716→        };
   717→
   718→        info!("  {workspace_id:<30} {status_icon}");
   719→    }
   720→}
   721→
   722→/// Detect git repo info from the current directory.
   723→fn detect_git_repo() -> Result<(PathBuf, String, String), String> {
   724→    // Get repo root
   725→    let root_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   726→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   727→        .output()
   728→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   729→    if !root_output.status.success() {
   730→        return Err("not inside a git repository".to_string());
   731→    }
   732→    let root = PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&root_output.stdout).trim());
   733→
   734→    // Get remote URL
   735→    let url_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   736→        .args(["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
   737→        .output()
   738→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   739→    let url = if url_output.status.success() {
   740→        String::from_utf8_lossy(&url_output.stdout)
   741→            .trim()
   742→            .to_string()
   743→    } else {
   744→        root.to_string_lossy().to_string() // local-only repo, use path as URL
   745→    };
   746→
   747→    // Get current branch
   748→    let branch_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   749→        .args(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"])
   750→        .output()
   751→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   752→    let branch = String::from_utf8_lossy(&branch_output.stdout)
   753→        .trim()
   754→        .to_string();
   755→
   756→    Ok((root, url, branch))
   757→}
   758→
   759→/// Derive a short repo name from a directory path.
   760→/// "/Users/jeevan/code/lightfast" → "lightfast"
   761→fn derive_repo_name(path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
   762→    path.file_name()
   763→        .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
   764→        .unwrap_or("repo")
   765→        .to_string()
   766→}

     1→use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     2→use std::process::Command;
     3→
     4→use crate::config;
     5→
     6→/// Check if a URL looks like a local filesystem path (vs a remote git URL).
     7→pub fn is_local_path(url: &str) -> bool {
     8→    // Local paths: start with /, ./, ../, or ~/ and don't contain ://
     9→    if url.contains("://") {
    10→        return false;
    11→    }
    12→    url.starts_with('/') || url.starts_with("./") || url.starts_with("../") || url.starts_with("~/")
    13→}
    14→
    15→/// Check if a workspace clone already exists on disk.
    16→pub fn workspace_exists(workspace_root: &Path, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> bool {
    17→    let dir = config::workspace_dir(workspace_root, repo, branch);
    18→    dir.join(".git").exists()
    19→}
    20→
    21→/// Create a full git clone for a workspace.
    22→///
    23→/// - Local paths use `git clone --local` for hardlink-based fast clones
    24→/// - Remote URLs use standard `git clone`
    25→/// - Branch is checked out via `-b` flag
    26→/// - Target directory is determined by workspace_root + repo + branch
    27→pub fn clone_workspace(
    28→    workspace_root: &Path,
    29→    repo: &str,
    30→    url: &str,
    31→    branch: &str,
    32→) -> Result<PathBuf, CloneError> {
    33→    let target_dir = config::workspace_dir(workspace_root, repo, branch);
    34→
    35→    // Don't re-clone if it already exists
    36→    if target_dir.join(".git").exists() {
    37→        return Ok(target_dir);
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    // Ensure parent directory exists
    41→    if let Some(parent) = target_dir.parent() {
    42→        std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)
    43→            .map_err(|e| CloneError::Filesystem(parent.to_path_buf(), e))?;
    44→    }
    45→
    46→    let mut cmd = Command::new("git");
    47→    cmd.arg("clone");
    48→
    49→    // Use --local for local paths (hardlinks, fast)
    50→    if is_local_path(url) {
    51→        cmd.arg("--local");
    52→    }
    53→
    54→    // Checkout the specified branch
    55→    cmd.arg("-b").arg(branch);
    56→
    57→    // Source and destination
    58→    cmd.arg(url).arg(&target_dir);
    59→
    60→    let output = cmd
    61→        .output()
    62→        .map_err(|e| CloneError::GitNotFound(e.to_string()))?;
    63→
    64→    if !output.status.success() {
    65→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
    66→        return Err(CloneError::GitFailed {
    67→            repo: repo.to_string(),
    68→            branch: branch.to_string(),
    69→            stderr,
    70→        });
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    Ok(target_dir)
    74→}
    75→
    76→/// Remove a workspace clone from disk.
    77→pub fn remove_workspace(workspace_root: &Path, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> Result<(), CloneError> {
    78→    let dir = config::workspace_dir(workspace_root, repo, branch);
    79→    if dir.exists() {
    80→        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).map_err(|e| CloneError::Filesystem(dir, e))?;
    81→    }
    82→    Ok(())
    83→}
    84→
    85→/// Build the git clone command arguments (for testing/debugging without executing).
    86→pub fn build_clone_args(url: &str, branch: &str, target: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    87→    let mut args = vec!["clone".to_string()];
    88→
    89→    if is_local_path(url) {
    90→        args.push("--local".to_string());
    91→    }
    92→
    93→    args.push("-b".to_string());
    94→    args.push(branch.to_string());
    95→    args.push(url.to_string());
    96→    args.push(target.to_string_lossy().to_string());
    97→
    98→    args
    99→}
   100→
   101→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   102→pub enum CloneError {
   103→    #[error("git not found: {0}")]
   104→    GitNotFound(String),
   105→
   106→    #[error("git clone failed for {repo}/{branch}: {stderr}")]
   107→    GitFailed {
   108→        repo: String,
   109→        branch: String,
   110→        stderr: String,
   111→    },
   112→
   113→    #[error("filesystem error at {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   114→    Filesystem(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   115→}

     5→const HINTS_FILENAME: &str = ".dual.toml";

     7→
     8→/// Shared configuration file propagation settings.
     9→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    10→pub struct SharedConfig {
    11→    /// Files and directories to share across workspaces.
    12→    /// e.g. [".vercel", ".env.local", ".env"]
    13→    #[serde(default)]
    14→    pub files: Vec<String>,
    15→}
    16→
    17→/// Per-repo runtime hints, read from .dual.toml in a workspace directory.
    18→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    19→pub struct RepoHints {
    20→    /// Docker image to use for containers (default: "node:20")
    21→    #[serde(default = "default_image")]
    22→    pub image: String,
    23→
    24→    /// Ports that services bind to inside the container
    25→    #[serde(default)]
    26→    pub ports: Vec<u16>,
    27→
    28→    /// Setup command to run after container creation (e.g. "pnpm install")
    29→    pub setup: Option<String>,
    30→
    31→    /// Environment variables for the container
    32→    #[serde(default)]
    33→    pub env: HashMap<String, String>,
    34→
    35→    /// Shared files to propagate across workspaces
    36→    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    37→    pub shared: Option<SharedConfig>,
    38→}
    39→
    40→fn default_image() -> String {
    41→    DEFAULT_IMAGE.to_string()
    42→}
    43→
    44→impl Default for RepoHints {
    45→    fn default() -> Self {
    46→        Self {
    47→            image: DEFAULT_IMAGE.to_string(),
    48→            ports: Vec::new(),
    49→            setup: None,
    50→            env: HashMap::new(),
    51→            shared: None,
    52→        }
    53→    }
    54→}
    55→
    56→/// Get the shared config directory for a repo: ~/.dual/shared/{repo}/
    57→pub fn shared_dir(repo: &str) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    58→    dirs::home_dir().map(|home| home.join(".dual").join("shared").join(repo))
    59→}
    60→
    61→/// Load RepoHints from a workspace directory's .dual.toml.
    62→/// Returns default hints if the file doesn't exist.
    63→pub fn load_hints(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<RepoHints, HintsError> {
    64→    let path = workspace_dir.join(HINTS_FILENAME);
    65→
    66→    if !path.exists() {
    67→        return Ok(RepoHints::default());
    68→    }
    69→
    70→    let contents =
    71→        std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| HintsError::ReadError(path.clone(), e))?;
    72→    let hints: RepoHints =
    73→        toml::from_str(&contents).map_err(|e| HintsError::ParseError(path, e))?;
    74→    Ok(hints)
    75→}
    76→
    77→/// Write RepoHints to a workspace directory's .dual.toml.
    78→pub fn write_hints(workspace_dir: &Path, hints: &RepoHints) -> Result<(), HintsError> {
    79→    let path = workspace_dir.join(HINTS_FILENAME);
    80→    let contents = toml::to_string_pretty(hints).map_err(HintsError::SerializeError)?;
    81→    std::fs::write(&path, contents).map_err(|e| HintsError::WriteError(path, e))?;
    82→    Ok(())
    83→}
    84→
    85→/// Parse hints from TOML string (for testing).
    86→pub fn parse_hints(toml_str: &str) -> Result<RepoHints, HintsError> {
    87→    let hints: RepoHints = toml::from_str(toml_str)
    88→        .map_err(|e| HintsError::ParseError(PathBuf::from("<string>"), e))?;
    89→    Ok(hints)
    90→}
    91→
    92→/// Compute the workspace identifier from repo + branch.
    93→/// e.g. ("lightfast", "feat/auth") → "lightfast-feat__auth"
    94→pub fn workspace_id(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String {
    95→    format!("{}-{}", repo, encode_branch(branch))
    96→}
    97→
    98→/// Get the workspace directory for a repo + branch combination.
    99→/// Layout: {workspace_root}/{repo}/{encoded_branch}/
   100→pub fn workspace_dir(workspace_root: &Path, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> PathBuf {
   101→    workspace_root.join(repo).join(encode_branch(branch))
   102→}
   103→
   104→/// Compute the container name for a repo + branch combination.
   105→/// Pattern: dual-{repo}-{encoded_branch}
   106→pub fn container_name(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String {
   107→    format!("dual-{}-{}", repo, encode_branch(branch))
   108→}
   109→
   110→/// Encode a branch name for filesystem use.
   111→/// Replaces `/` with `__` (double underscore).
   112→/// e.g. "feat/auth" → "feat__auth"
   113→pub fn encode_branch(branch: &str) -> String {
   114→    branch.replace('/', "__")
   115→}
   116→
   117→/// Decode an encoded branch name back to the original.
   118→/// Replaces `__` with `/`.
   119→/// e.g. "feat__auth" → "feat/auth"
   120→pub fn decode_branch(encoded: &str) -> String {
   121→    encoded.replace("__", "/")
   122→}
   123→
   124→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   125→pub enum HintsError {
   126→    #[error("Failed to read {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   127→    ReadError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   128→
   129→    #[error("Failed to write {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   130→    WriteError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   131→
   132→    #[error("Failed to parse {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   133→    ParseError(PathBuf, toml::de::Error),
   134→
   135→    #[error("Failed to serialize hints: {0}")]
   136→    SerializeError(toml::ser::Error),
   137→}

     1→use std::path::Path;
     2→use std::process::Command;
     3→
     4→const WORKSPACE_MOUNT: &str = "/workspace";
     5→
     6→/// Container status.
     7→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
     8→pub enum ContainerStatus {
     9→    Running,
    10→    Stopped,
    11→    Missing,
    12→}
    13→
    14→/// Create a new Docker container for a workspace.
    15→///
    16→/// - Bind mounts workspace dir to /workspace
    17→/// - Anonymous volume for node_modules isolation
    18→/// - Sets working directory to /workspace
    19→/// - Uses bridge network (default) for network namespace isolation
    20→pub fn create(name: &str, workspace_dir: &Path, image: &str) -> Result<String, ContainerError> {
    21→    let output = Command::new("docker")
    22→        .args(build_create_args(name, workspace_dir, image))
    23→        .output()
    24→        .map_err(|e| ContainerError::DockerNotFound(e.to_string()))?;
    25→
    26→    if !output.status.success() {
    27→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
    28→        return Err(ContainerError::Failed {
    29→            operation: "create".to_string(),
    30→            name: name.to_string(),
    31→            stderr,
    32→        });
    33→    }
    34→
    35→    Ok(name.to_string())
    36→}
    37→
    38→/// Start an existing container.
    39→pub fn start(name: &str) -> Result<(), ContainerError> {
    40→    docker_simple("start", name)
    41→}
    42→
    43→/// Stop a running container.
    44→pub fn stop(name: &str) -> Result<(), ContainerError> {
    45→    docker_simple("stop", name)
    46→}
    47→
    48→/// Remove a container (must be stopped first).
    49→pub fn destroy(name: &str) -> Result<(), ContainerError> {
    50→    docker_simple("rm", name)
    51→}
    52→
    53→/// Execute a command inside a running container.
    54→///
    55→/// Returns the exit code of the command.
    56→pub fn exec(name: &str, cmd: &[&str], tty: bool) -> Result<i32, ContainerError> {
    57→    let mut args = vec!["exec".to_string()];
    58→
    59→    if tty {
    60→        args.push("-t".to_string());
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    args.push("-w".to_string());
    64→    args.push(WORKSPACE_MOUNT.to_string());
    65→    args.push(name.to_string());
    66→    args.extend(cmd.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()));
    67→
    68→    let status = Command::new("docker")
    69→        .args(&args)
    70→        .status()
    71→        .map_err(|e| ContainerError::DockerNotFound(e.to_string()))?;
    72→
    73→    Ok(status.code().unwrap_or(1))
    74→}
    75→
    76→/// Check the status of a container.
    77→pub fn status(name: &str) -> ContainerStatus {
    78→    let output = Command::new("docker")
    79→        .args(["inspect", "--format", "{{.State.Running}}", name])
    80→        .output();
    81→
    82→    match output {
    83→        Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {
    84→            let running = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string();
    85→            if running == "true" {
    86→                ContainerStatus::Running
    87→            } else {
    88→                ContainerStatus::Stopped
    89→            }
    90→        }
    91→        _ => ContainerStatus::Missing,
    92→    }
    93→}
    94→
    95→/// Get the IP address of a running container on the Docker bridge network.
    96→pub fn get_ip(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    97→    let output = Command::new("docker")
    98→        .args([
    99→            "inspect",
   100→            "--format",
   101→            "{{range .NetworkSettings.Networks}}{{.IPAddress}}{{end}}",
   102→            name,
   103→        ])
   104→        .output()
   105→        .ok()?;
   106→
   107→    if !output.status.success() {
   108→        return None;
   109→    }
   110→
   111→    let ip = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim().to_string();
   112→    if ip.is_empty() { None } else { Some(ip) }
   113→}
   114→
   115→/// List all dual-managed containers (name and running status).
   116→pub fn list_all() -> Vec<(String, bool)> {
   117→    let output = Command::new("docker")
   118→        .args([
   119→            "ps",
   120→            "-a",
   121→            "--filter",
   122→            "name=dual-",
   123→            "--format",
   124→            "{{.Names}}\t{{.State}}",
   125→        ])
   126→        .output();
   127→
   128→    match output {
   129→        Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {
   130→            let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
   131→            stdout
   132→                .lines()
   133→                .filter(|line| !line.is_empty())
   134→                .filter_map(|line| {
   135→                    let mut parts = line.splitn(2, '\t');
   136→                    let name = parts.next()?.to_string();
   137→                    let state = parts.next().unwrap_or("");
   138→                    Some((name, state == "running"))
   139→                })
   140→                .collect()
   141→        }
   142→        _ => Vec::new(),
   143→    }
   144→}
   145→
   146→/// Build the docker create arguments (for testing).
   147→pub fn build_create_args(name: &str, workspace_dir: &Path, image: &str) -> Vec<String> {
   148→    vec![
   149→        "create".to_string(),
   150→        "--name".to_string(),
   151→        name.to_string(),
   152→        // Bind mount workspace
   153→        "-v".to_string(),
   154→        format!("{}:{WORKSPACE_MOUNT}", workspace_dir.display()),
   155→        // Anonymous volume for node_modules isolation
   156→        "-v".to_string(),
   157→        format!("{WORKSPACE_MOUNT}/node_modules"),
   158→        // Working directory
   159→        "-w".to_string(),
   160→        WORKSPACE_MOUNT.to_string(),
   161→        // Image
   162→        image.to_string(),
   163→        // Keep container running for docker exec
   164→        "sleep".to_string(),
   165→        "infinity".to_string(),
   166→    ]
   167→}
   168→
   169→/// Build docker exec arguments (for testing).
   170→pub fn build_exec_args(name: &str, cmd: &[&str], tty: bool) -> Vec<String> {
   171→    let mut args = vec!["exec".to_string()];
   172→    if tty {
   173→        args.push("-t".to_string());
   174→    }
   175→    args.push("-w".to_string());
   176→    args.push(WORKSPACE_MOUNT.to_string());
   177→    args.push(name.to_string());
   178→    args.extend(cmd.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()));
   179→    args
   180→}
   181→
   182→fn docker_simple(operation: &str, name: &str) -> Result<(), ContainerError> {
   183→    let output = Command::new("docker")
   184→        .args([operation, name])
   185→        .output()
   186→        .map_err(|e| ContainerError::DockerNotFound(e.to_string()))?;
   187→
   188→    if !output.status.success() {
   189→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
   190→        return Err(ContainerError::Failed {
   191→            operation: operation.to_string(),
   192→            name: name.to_string(),
   193→            stderr,
   194→        });
   195→    }
   196→
   197→    Ok(())
   198→}
   199→
   200→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   201→pub enum ContainerError {
   202→    #[error("docker not found: {0}")]
   203→    DockerNotFound(String),
   204→
   205→    #[error("docker {operation} failed for {name}: {stderr}")]
   206→    Failed {
   207→        operation: String,
   208→        name: String,
   209→        stderr: String,
   210→    },
   211→}
   212→
   213→#[cfg(test)]
   214→mod tests {
   215→    use super::*;
   216→
   217→    #[test]
   218→    fn create_args_correct() {
   219→        let args = build_create_args(
   220→            "dual-lightfast-main",
   221→            Path::new("/home/user/dual-workspaces/lightfast/main"),
   222→            "node:20",
   223→        );
   224→        assert_eq!(args[0], "create");
   225→        assert_eq!(args[1], "--name");
   226→        assert_eq!(args[2], "dual-lightfast-main");
   227→        assert_eq!(args[3], "-v");
   228→        assert!(args[4].contains("/home/user/dual-workspaces/lightfast/main:/workspace"));
   229→        assert_eq!(args[5], "-v");
   230→        assert_eq!(args[6], "/workspace/node_modules");
   231→        assert_eq!(args[7], "-w");
   232→        assert_eq!(args[8], "/workspace");
   233→        assert_eq!(args[9], "node:20");
   234→        assert_eq!(args[10], "sleep");
   235→        assert_eq!(args[11], "infinity");
   236→    }
   237→
   238→    #[test]
   239→    fn exec_args_without_tty() {
   240→        let args = build_exec_args("dual-lightfast-main", &["pnpm", "dev"], false);
   241→        assert_eq!(
   242→            args,

   244→                "exec",
   245→                "-w",
   246→                "/workspace",
   247→                "dual-lightfast-main",
   248→                "pnpm",

   250→            ]
   251→        );
   252→    }
   253→
   254→    #[test]
   255→    fn exec_args_with_tty() {

     1→/// Default commands that route to the container.
     2→const CONTAINER_COMMANDS: &[&str] = &[
     3→    "npm", "npx", "pnpm", "node", "python", "python3", "pip", "pip3", "curl", "make",
     4→];
     5→
     6→/// Where a command should execute.
     7→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
     8→pub enum RouteTarget {
     9→    Container,
    10→    Host,
    11→}
    12→
    13→/// Classify a command as host or container.
    14→///
    15→/// Default rules from SPEC.md:
    16→/// - npm/pnpm/node/python/curl etc → container
    17→/// - Everything else → host
    18→pub fn classify(command: &str) -> RouteTarget {
    19→    // Extract the base command name (strip path prefix)
    20→    let base = command.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(command);
    21→
    22→    if CONTAINER_COMMANDS.contains(&base) {
    23→        RouteTarget::Container
    24→    } else {
    25→        RouteTarget::Host
    26→    }
    27→}
    28→
    29→/// Generate shell RC content that intercepts runtime commands for a workspace.
    30→///
    31→/// The generated content defines shell functions that wrap `docker exec`
    32→/// for each command that should route to the container. These functions
    33→/// handle TTY detection automatically.
    34→///
    35→/// # Arguments
    36→/// * `container_name` - The Docker container name (e.g. "dual-lightfast-main")
    37→pub fn generate_rc(container_name: &str) -> String {
    38→    let mut rc = String::new();
    39→
    40→    rc.push_str("# Generated by dual — do not edit\n");
    41→    rc.push_str(&format!(
    42→        "# Shell interceptors for container: {container_name}\n\n"
    43→    ));
    44→
    45→    // Set the container name as an environment variable for reference
    46→    rc.push_str(&format!("export DUAL_CONTAINER=\"{container_name}\"\n\n"));
    47→
    48→    // Generate a function for each container command
    49→    for cmd in CONTAINER_COMMANDS {
    50→        rc.push_str(&generate_function(cmd, container_name));
    51→        rc.push('\n');
    52→    }
    53→
    54→    rc
    55→}
    56→
    57→/// Generate a single shell function that intercepts a command.
    58→///
    59→/// The function:
    60→/// - Detects if stdout is a TTY and passes -t to docker exec if so
    61→/// - Forwards all arguments to the command inside the container
    62→/// - Preserves exit codes
    63→fn generate_function(command: &str, container_name: &str) -> String {
    64→    format!(
    65→        r#"{command}() {{
    66→    if [ -t 1 ]; then
    67→        command docker exec -t -w /workspace {container_name} {command} "$@"
    68→    else
    69→        command docker exec -w /workspace {container_name} {command} "$@"
    70→    fi
    71→}}
    72→"#
    73→    )
    74→}
    75→
    76→/// Generate a shell command to source the RC content from a string.
    77→/// Useful for injecting into tmux pane creation.
    78→pub fn source_command(container_name: &str) -> String {
    79→    format!("eval \"$(dual shell-rc {container_name})\"")
    80→}
    81→
    82→/// Write the shell RC to a file and return the path.
    83→/// RC files are written to ~/.config/dual/rc/{container_name}.sh
    84→pub fn write_rc_file(container_name: &str) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf, std::io::Error> {
    85→    let rc_dir = dirs::config_dir()
    86→        .unwrap_or_else(|| std::path::PathBuf::from(".config"))
    87→        .join("dual")
    88→        .join("rc");
    89→    std::fs::create_dir_all(&rc_dir)?;
    90→
    91→    let rc_path = rc_dir.join(format!("{container_name}.sh"));
    92→    let rc_content = generate_rc(container_name);
    93→    std::fs::write(&rc_path, rc_content)?;
    94→
    95→    Ok(rc_path)
    96→}
    97→
    98→/// Get the source command for an RC file path.
    99→pub fn source_file_command(rc_path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
   100→    format!("source {}", rc_path.display())
   101→}
   102→
   103→#[cfg(test)]
   104→mod tests {
   105→    use super::*;
   106→
   107→    #[test]
   108→    fn classify_container_commands() {
   109→        assert_eq!(classify("npm"), RouteTarget::Container);
   110→        assert_eq!(classify("npx"), RouteTarget::Container);
   111→        assert_eq!(classify("pnpm"), RouteTarget::Container);
   112→        assert_eq!(classify("node"), RouteTarget::Container);
   113→        assert_eq!(classify("python"), RouteTarget::Container);
   114→        assert_eq!(classify("python3"), RouteTarget::Container);
   115→        assert_eq!(classify("pip"), RouteTarget::Container);
   116→        assert_eq!(classify("curl"), RouteTarget::Container);
   117→        assert_eq!(classify("make"), RouteTarget::Container);
   118→    }
   119→
   120→    #[test]
   121→    fn classify_host_commands() {
   122→        assert_eq!(classify("git"), RouteTarget::Host);
   123→        assert_eq!(classify("cat"), RouteTarget::Host);
   124→        assert_eq!(classify("ls"), RouteTarget::Host);
   125→        assert_eq!(classify("vim"), RouteTarget::Host);
   126→        assert_eq!(classify("nvim"), RouteTarget::Host);
   127→        assert_eq!(classify("ssh"), RouteTarget::Host);
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    #[test]
   131→    fn classify_strips_path_prefix() {
   132→        assert_eq!(classify("/usr/bin/npm"), RouteTarget::Container);
   133→        assert_eq!(classify("/usr/local/bin/node"), RouteTarget::Container);
   134→        assert_eq!(classify("/usr/bin/git"), RouteTarget::Host);
   135→    }
   136→
   137→    #[test]
   138→    fn generate_rc_contains_functions() {
   139→        let rc = generate_rc("dual-lightfast-main");
   140→
   141→        // Should contain the container name export
   142→        assert!(rc.contains("export DUAL_CONTAINER=\"dual-lightfast-main\""));
   143→
   144→        // Should contain functions for all container commands
   145→        assert!(rc.contains("npm()"));
   146→        assert!(rc.contains("npx()"));
   147→        assert!(rc.contains("pnpm()"));
   148→        assert!(rc.contains("node()"));
   149→        assert!(rc.contains("python()"));
   150→        assert!(rc.contains("curl()"));
   151→    }
   152→
   153→    #[test]
   154→    fn generate_rc_has_tty_detection() {
   155→        let rc = generate_rc("dual-lightfast-main");
   156→        assert!(rc.contains("if [ -t 1 ]; then"));
   157→        assert!(rc.contains("docker exec -t"));
   158→    }
   159→
   160→    #[test]
   161→    fn generate_rc_uses_correct_container() {
   162→        let rc = generate_rc("dual-agent-os-feat__memory");
   163→        assert!(rc.contains("dual-agent-os-feat__memory"));
   164→        // Every function should reference this container
   165→        for cmd in CONTAINER_COMMANDS {
   166→            assert!(rc.contains(&format!(
   167→                "command docker exec -t -w /workspace dual-agent-os-feat__memory {cmd}"
   168→            )));
   169→        }
   170→    }
   171→
   172→    #[test]
   173→    fn generated_function_preserves_args() {
   174→        let func = generate_function("npm", "dual-test");
   175→        assert!(func.contains("\"$@\""));
   176→    }
   177→
   178→    #[test]
   179→    fn source_command_format() {
   180→        let cmd = source_command("dual-lightfast-main");
   181→        assert_eq!(cmd, "eval \"$(dual shell-rc dual-lightfast-main)\"");
   182→    }
   183→
   184→    #[test]
   185→    fn write_rc_file_creates_file() {
   186→        let path = write_rc_file("dual-test-write-rc").unwrap();
   187→        assert!(path.exists());
   188→        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
   189→        assert!(content.contains("export DUAL_CONTAINER=\"dual-test-write-rc\""));
   190→        assert!(content.contains("npm()"));
   191→        // Clean up
   192→        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&path);

   198→        let cmd = source_file_command(path);
   199→        assert_eq!(cmd, "source /home/user/.config/dual/rc/dual-test.sh");

     1→use std::path::Path;
     2→use std::process::Command;
     3→
     4→/// Session name prefix for dual-managed sessions.
     5→const SESSION_PREFIX: &str = "dual-";
     6→
     7→/// Check if tmux is available on the system.
     8→pub fn is_available() -> bool {
     9→    Command::new("tmux")
    10→        .arg("-V")
    11→        .output()
    12→        .is_ok_and(|o| o.status.success())
    13→}
    14→
    15→/// Create a new detached tmux session for a workspace.
    16→///
    17→/// - Session name follows the container naming convention: dual-{repo}-{branch}
    18→/// - Working directory is set to the workspace clone dir
    19→/// - Optionally sources shell RC for command interception
    20→pub fn create_session(
    21→    session_name: &str,
    22→    cwd: &Path,
    23→    shell_rc: Option<&str>,
    24→) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    25→    // Create detached session with CWD
    26→    let output = Command::new("tmux")
    27→        .args(build_new_session_args(session_name, cwd))
    28→        .output()
    29→        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;
    30→
    31→    if !output.status.success() {
    32→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
    33→        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {
    34→            operation: "new-session".to_string(),
    35→            session: session_name.to_string(),
    36→            stderr,
    37→        });
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    // Source shell RC if provided
    41→    if let Some(rc) = shell_rc {
    42→        send_keys(session_name, rc)?;
    43→    }
    44→
    45→    Ok(())
    46→}
    47→
    48→/// Attach to an existing tmux session.
    49→pub fn attach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    50→    let status = Command::new("tmux")
    51→        .args(["attach-session", "-t", session_name])
    52→        .status()
    53→        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;
    54→
    55→    if !status.success() {
    56→        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {
    57→            operation: "attach-session".to_string(),
    58→            session: session_name.to_string(),
    59→            stderr: format!("exit code: {}", status.code().unwrap_or(-1)),
    60→        });
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    Ok(())
    64→}
    65→
    66→/// Detach the current client from a session.
    67→pub fn detach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    68→    tmux_simple(&["detach-client", "-s", session_name])
    69→}
    70→
    71→/// Destroy a tmux session (kill all panes and processes).
    72→pub fn destroy(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    73→    tmux_simple(&["kill-session", "-t", session_name])
    74→}
    75→
    76→/// Check if a session exists and is alive.
    77→pub fn is_alive(session_name: &str) -> bool {
    78→    Command::new("tmux")
    79→        .args(["has-session", "-t", session_name])
    80→        .output()
    81→        .is_ok_and(|o| o.status.success())
    82→}
    83→
    84→/// List all dual-managed tmux sessions.
    85→/// Returns session names that start with the dual- prefix.
    86→pub fn list_sessions() -> Vec<String> {
    87→    let output = Command::new("tmux")
    88→        .args(["list-sessions", "-F", "#{session_name}"])
    89→        .output();
    90→
    91→    match output {
    92→        Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {
    93→            let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    94→            stdout
    95→                .lines()
    96→                .filter(|line| line.starts_with(SESSION_PREFIX))
    97→                .map(|s| s.to_string())
    98→                .collect()
    99→        }
   100→        _ => Vec::new(),
   101→    }
   102→}
   103→
   104→/// Send keys (a command string) to a tmux session pane.
   105→pub fn send_keys(session_name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
   106→    tmux_simple(&["send-keys", "-t", session_name, keys, "Enter"])
   107→}
   108→
   109→/// Build the arguments for tmux new-session (for testing).
   110→pub fn build_new_session_args(session_name: &str, cwd: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
   111→    vec![
   112→        "new-session".to_string(),
   113→        "-d".to_string(),
   114→        "-s".to_string(),
   115→        session_name.to_string(),
   116→        "-c".to_string(),
   117→        cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   118→    ]
   119→}
   120→
   121→/// Generate the session name for a workspace.
   122→pub fn session_name(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String {
   123→    use crate::config::encode_branch;
   124→    format!("{SESSION_PREFIX}{repo}-{}", encode_branch(branch))
   125→}
   126→
   127→fn tmux_simple(args: &[&str]) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
   128→    let output = Command::new("tmux")
   129→        .args(args)
   130→        .output()
   131→        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;
   132→
   133→    if !output.status.success() {
   134→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
   135→        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {
   136→            operation: args.first().unwrap_or(&"unknown").to_string(),
   137→            session: args
   138→                .iter()
   139→                .position(|&a| a == "-t" || a == "-s")
   140→                .and_then(|i| args.get(i + 1))
   141→                .unwrap_or(&"unknown")
   142→                .to_string(),
   143→            stderr,
   144→        });
   145→    }
   146→
   147→    Ok(())
   148→}
   149→
   150→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   151→pub enum TmuxError {
   152→    #[error("tmux not found: {0}")]
   153→    NotFound(String),
   154→
   155→    #[error("tmux {operation} failed for {session}: {stderr}")]
   156→    Failed {
   157→        operation: String,
   158→        session: String,
   159→        stderr: String,
   160→    },
   161→}
   162→
   163→#[cfg(test)]
   164→mod tests {
   165→    use super::*;
   166→    use std::path::Path;
   167→
   168→    #[test]
   169→    fn new_session_args_correct() {
   170→        let args = build_new_session_args(
   171→            "dual-lightfast-main",
   172→            Path::new("/home/user/dual-workspaces/lightfast/main"),
   173→        );
   174→        assert_eq!(
   175→            args,
   176→            vec![
   177→                "new-session",
   178→                "-d",
   179→                "-s",
   180→                "dual-lightfast-main",
   181→                "-c",
   182→                "/home/user/dual-workspaces/lightfast/main",
   183→            ]
   184→        );
   185→    }
   186→
   187→    #[test]
   188→    fn session_name_format() {
   189→        assert_eq!(session_name("lightfast", "main"), "dual-lightfast-main");
   190→        assert_eq!(
   191→            session_name("lightfast", "feat/auth"),
   192→            "dual-lightfast-feat__auth"
   193→        );
   194→        assert_eq!(
   195→            session_name("agent-os", "v2-rewrite"),
   196→            "dual-agent-os-v2-rewrite"
   197→        );
   198→    }
   199→
   200→    #[test]
   201→    fn session_name_matches_container_name() {
   202→        // Session names should match container names for consistency
   203→        use crate::config;
   204→        assert_eq!(
   205→            session_name("lightfast", "main"),
   206→            config::container_name("lightfast", "main")
   207→        );
   208→        assert_eq!(
   209→            session_name("lightfast", "feat/auth"),
   210→            config::container_name("lightfast", "feat/auth")
   211→        );
   212→    }
   213→
   214→    #[test]
   215→    fn session_prefix_is_dual() {
   216→        assert_eq!(SESSION_PREFIX, "dual-");
   217→    }
   218→
   219→    #[test]
   220→    fn tmux_availability_check_runs() {
   221→        // This test just verifies the function doesn't panic
   222→        // It may return true or false depending on the system
   223→        let _available = is_available();

     1→use clap::{Parser, Subcommand};
     2→
     3→#[derive(Parser)]
     4→#[command(
     5→    name = "dual",
     6→    version,
     7→    about = "Terminal workspace orchestrator for parallel multi-repo development"
     8→)]
     9→pub struct Cli {
    10→    #[command(subcommand)]
    11→    pub command: Option<Command>,
    12→}
    13→
    14→#[derive(Subcommand)]
    15→pub enum Command {
    16→    /// Register the current repo as a dual workspace
    17→    Add {
    18→        /// Short name for the repo (derived from directory name if omitted)
    19→        #[arg(short, long)]
    20→        name: Option<String>,
    21→    },
    22→
    23→    /// Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo
    24→    Create {
    25→        /// Repo name (must already be added)
    26→        repo: String,
    27→
    28→        /// Branch name
    29→        branch: String,
    30→    },
    31→
    32→    /// Launch a workspace (clone, container, tmux session)
    33→    Launch {
    34→        /// Workspace to launch (e.g. lightfast-main)
    35→        workspace: String,
    36→    },
    37→
    38→    /// List all workspaces and their status
    39→    List,
    40→
    41→    /// Destroy a workspace (stop container, remove clone)
    42→    Destroy {
    43→        /// Workspace to destroy (e.g. lightfast-feat__auth)
    44→        workspace: String,
    45→    },
    46→
    47→    /// Open all services for a workspace in the browser
    48→    Open {
    49→        /// Workspace to open (defaults to current)
    50→        workspace: Option<String>,
    51→    },
    52→
    53→    /// List running workspace URLs
    54→    Urls {
    55→        /// Workspace to show URLs for (defaults to all)
    56→        workspace: Option<String>,
    57→    },
    58→
    59→    /// Sync shared config files for current workspace
    60→    Sync {
    61→        /// Workspace to sync (detected from current directory if omitted)
    62→        workspace: Option<String>,
    63→    },
    64→
    65→    /// Start the reverse proxy for browser access
    66→    Proxy,
    67→
    68→    /// Output shell RC for a container (used internally)
    69→    #[command(name = "shell-rc", hide = true)]
    70→    ShellRc {
    71→        /// Container name
    72→        container: String,
    73→    },
    74→}
    75→

  "summary": "Terminal workspace orchestrator for parallel multi-repo development. v2.0.0 shipped.",
  "description": "Dual: terminal workspace orchestrator for parallel multi-repo development with AI coding agents. Manages isolated development environments — one full git clone per workspace — so a developer can run multiple repos x multiple branches simultaneously, all with Claude Code sessions active, all running dev servers on default ports, with zero conflicts.\n\n**v2.0.0 shipped** (Feb 15, 2026). Working features: CLI (add, create, launch, list, destroy, open, urls, sync, proxy, shell-rc), state management with atomic writes + file locking, git clone lifecycle, Docker container lifecycle, shell command routing via RC file generation, tmux session management, reverse proxy with subdomain routing, shared config propagation, production readiness (thiserror, atomic state, tracing).\n\nBuilt in Rust (edition 2024).",
  "state": "started"
}
