   124→
   125→## Phase 1: v2.1.0 — Bug Fixes & Completions
   126→
   127→### Overview
   128→Fix the three fundamental bugs and complete unimplemented features. All changes are within existing modules — no new modules, no new dependencies.
   129→
   130→### Changes Required:
   131→
   132→#### 1. Fix Branch Model (Bug 1)
   133→**Files**: `src/clone.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   134→
   135→**Current behavior**: `git clone -b <branch>` fails when branch doesn't exist at origin.
   136→
   137→**New behavior**: Clone from local main workspace using `--local`, then `git checkout -b <new-branch>`.
   138→
   139→**Implementation**:
   140→
   141→In `src/clone.rs`, change `clone_workspace()`:
   142→```rust
   143→// Instead of:
   144→//   git clone -b <branch> <url> <target_dir>
   145→// Do:
   146→//   1. Find local main workspace path for this repo
   147→//   2. git clone --local <main_workspace_path> <target_dir>
   148→//   3. cd <target_dir> && git checkout -b <branch>
   149→```
   150→
   151→In `src/main.rs`, update `cmd_create()`:
   152→- Look up the main workspace (the one with `path: Some(...)`) for the given repo
   153→- Pass its path to a new `clone_from_local()` function
   154→- If no main workspace exists, fall back to `git clone <url>` then `git checkout -b`
   155→
   156→New function signature in `clone.rs`:
   157→```rust
   158→pub fn clone_from_local(
   159→    main_workspace_path: &Path,
   160→    target_dir: &Path,
   161→    new_branch: &str,
   162→) -> Result<(), CloneError>
   163→```
   164→
   165→**Tests to add**:
   166→- `test_clone_from_local_args` — verify git clone --local + git checkout -b args
   167→- `test_clone_fallback_to_remote` — verify fallback when no local main exists
   168→- Update existing `test_clone_workspace_args` — adjust for new behavior
   169→
   170→#### 2. Fix Default `.dual.toml` (Bug 3)
   171→**File**: `src/config.rs`
   172→
   173→**Current behavior**: `toml::to_string_pretty()` generates bare TOML without comments.
   174→
   175→**New behavior**: Hand-write a commented template string instead of serializing.
   176→
   177→**Implementation**:
   178→
   179→Replace `write_hints()` to write a hand-crafted template:
   180→```rust
   181→pub fn write_default_hints(repo_root: &Path) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {
   182→    let template = r#"# Dual workspace configuration
   183→# See: https://github.com/jeevanpillay/dual
   184→
   185→# Docker image for the container runtime
   186→image = "node:20"
   187→
   188→# Ports your dev server uses (for reverse proxy routing)
   189→# Example: ports = [3000, 3001]
   190→# ports = []
   191→
   192→# Shell command to run after container creation (e.g., dependency install)
   193→# Example: setup = "pnpm install"
   194→# setup = ""
   195→
   196→# Environment variables passed to the container
   197→# Example:
   198→# [env]
   199→# NODE_ENV = "development"
   200→
   201→# Commands to route to the container (in addition to defaults)
   202→# Default: npm, npx, pnpm, node, python, python3, pip, pip3, curl, make
   203→# Example: extra_commands = ["cargo", "go", "ruby"]
   204→# extra_commands = []
   205→
   206→# Directories to isolate with anonymous Docker volumes
   207→# These directories get their own volume so they don't sync between host/container
   208→# Example: anonymous_volumes = ["node_modules", ".next", "target"]
   209→# anonymous_volumes = ["node_modules"]
   210→
   211→# Files to share across all workspaces of this repo
   212→# These are gitignored files that should be available in every branch workspace
   213→# [shared]
   214→# files = [".env.local", ".vercel"]
   215→"#;
   216→    let hints_path = repo_root.join(".dual.toml");
   217→    std::fs::write(&hints_path, template)?;
   218→    Ok(())
   219→}
   220→```
   221→
   222→Update `cmd_add()` in `src/main.rs:101-111` to call `write_default_hints()` instead of `write_hints(&RepoHints::default())`.
   223→
   224→Keep `write_hints()` for programmatic updates — it serializes actual `RepoHints` structs.
   225→
   226→#### 3. Wire `env` Vars to Container Creation
   227→**Files**: `src/container.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   228→
   229→**Current behavior**: `env` HashMap loaded at `main.rs:262` then dropped. `container::create()` takes only `(name, dir, image)`.
   230→
   231→**Implementation**:
   232→
   233→Update `container::create()` signature:
   234→```rust
   235→pub fn create(
   236→    name: &str,
   237→    workspace_dir: &Path,
   238→    image: &str,
   239→    env: &HashMap<String, String>,
   240→    anonymous_volumes: &[String],
   241→) -> Result<(), ContainerError>
   242→```
   243→
   244→In `build_create_args()`, add env vars:
   245→```rust
   246→for (key, value) in env {
   247→    args.push("-e".to_string());
   248→    args.push(format!("{key}={value}"));
   249→}
   250→```
   251→
   252→Replace hardcoded `node_modules` volume with configurable `anonymous_volumes`:
   253→```rust
   254→for vol in anonymous_volumes {
   255→    args.push("-v".to_string());
   256→    args.push(format!("/workspace/{vol}"));
   257→}
   258→```
   259→
   260→Update `cmd_launch()` in `main.rs` to pass `hints.env` and `hints.anonymous_volumes` to `container::create()`.
   261→
   262→#### 4. Wire `setup` Command Execution
   263→**Files**: `src/container.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   264→
   265→**Current behavior**: `setup` field exists in `RepoHints` but is never executed.
   266→
   267→**Implementation**:
   268→
   269→Add to `container.rs`:
   270→```rust
   271→pub fn exec_setup(name: &str, setup_cmd: &str) -> Result<(), ContainerError> {
   272→    // docker exec <name> sh -c "<setup_cmd>"
   273→}
   274→```
   275→
   276→In `cmd_launch()`, after `container::start()` and before tmux session creation:
   277→```rust
   278→if let Some(setup) = &hints.setup {
   279→    info!("Running setup: {setup}");
   280→    container::exec_setup(&container_name, setup)?;
   281→}
   282→```
   283→
   284→#### 5. Configurable Container Commands
   285→**Files**: `src/config.rs`, `src/shell.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   286→
   287→**Current behavior**: `CONTAINER_COMMANDS` at `shell.rs:2-4` is a hardcoded `&[&str]`.
   288→
   289→**Implementation**:
   290→
   291→Add to `RepoHints` in `config.rs`:
   292→```rust
   293→#[serde(default)]
   294→pub extra_commands: Vec<String>,
   295→
   296→#[serde(default)]
   297→pub anonymous_volumes: Vec<String>,
   298→```
   299→
   300→Update `Default` impl:
   301→```rust
   302→extra_commands: Vec::new(),
   303→anonymous_volumes: vec!["node_modules".to_string()],
   304→```
   305→
   306→Update `shell.rs` to accept additional commands:
   307→```rust
   308→pub fn write_rc_file(
   309→    container_name: &str,
   310→    extra_commands: &[String],
   311→) -> Result<PathBuf, ShellError>
   312→```
   313→
   314→Merge `CONTAINER_COMMANDS` with `extra_commands` when generating RC.
   315→
   316→#### 6. Update `cmd_launch()` Pipeline
   317→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   318→
   319→Wire all the new parameters through the launch pipeline:
   320→```
   321→load hints → pass hints.env + hints.anonymous_volumes to container::create()
   322→           → pass hints.setup to container::exec_setup()
   323→           → pass hints.extra_commands to shell::write_rc_file()
   324→```
   325→

   349→
   350→## Phase 2: v2.2.0 — Context Awareness & UX
   351→
   352→### Overview
   353→Make the CLI context-aware so commands auto-detect the current repo/workspace from the working directory. Fix tmux nested session handling.
   354→
   355→### Changes Required:
   356→
   357→#### 1. Context-Aware `dual create`
   358→**Files**: `src/cli.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   359→
   360→Make `repo` optional in `Create` command:
   361→```rust
   362→Create {
   363→    /// Branch name
   364→    branch: String,
   365→    /// Repo name (auto-detected if omitted)
   366→    #[arg(long)]
   367→    repo: Option<String>,
   368→},
   369→```
   370→
   371→In `cmd_create()`, if `repo` is `None`:
   372→1. Call `detect_git_repo()` to get the current repo info
   373→2. Look up the repo name from state by matching the git remote URL
   374→3. If found, use it. If not, error with a helpful message.
   375→
   376→This means: `dual create feat/auth` works when inside a repo directory.
   377→
   378→#### 2. Context-Aware `launch`, `destroy`, `open`, `urls`
   379→**Files**: `src/cli.rs`, `src/main.rs`
   380→
   381→Make the workspace arg optional for these commands:
   382→```rust
   383→Launch {
   384→    /// Workspace identifier (auto-detected if omitted)
   385→    workspace: Option<String>,
   386→},
   387→Destroy {
   388→    workspace: Option<String>,
   389→},
   390→```
   391→
   392→When workspace is `None`, call `detect_workspace()` to find the current workspace.
   393→
   394→#### 3. Tmux Nested Session Detection
   395→**File**: `src/tmux.rs`
   396→
   397→Add detection in `attach()`:
   398→```rust
   399→pub fn attach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
   400→    if std::env::var("TMUX").is_ok() {
   401→        // Already in tmux — use switch-client instead of attach
   402→        // tmux switch-client -t <session_name>
   403→    } else {
   404→        // Not in tmux — use attach-session
   405→        // tmux attach-session -t <session_name>
   406→    }
   407→}
   408→```
   409→
   410→#### 4. Improve `dual list` Output
   411→**File**: `src/main.rs`
   412→
   413→Group workspaces by repo in the list output:
   414→```
   415→lightfast
   416→  main          ● running    (container: up, tmux: attached)
   417→  feat/auth     ○ stopped    (container: stopped, tmux: none)
   418→  feat/billing  ◌ lazy       (not cloned yet)
   419→
   420→agent-os
   421→  main          ● running    (container: up, tmux: background)
   422→```
   423→
   424→### Success Criteria:
   425→
   426→#### Automated Verification:
   427→- [ ] `cargo test` — all tests pass
   428→- [ ] `cargo clippy` — no warnings
   429→- [ ] New tests for context detection in create pass
   430→- [ ] New tests for optional workspace args pass
   431→- [ ] New tests for `$TMUX` detection pass
   432→- [ ] New tests for grouped list output pass
   433→
   434→#### Manual Verification:
   435→- [ ] `cd ~/code/myproject && dual create feat/auth` auto-detects repo
   436→- [ ] `cd ~/code/myproject && dual launch` auto-detects workspace
   437→- [ ] Running `dual launch` from inside tmux uses `switch-client` (no nesting)
   438→- [ ] `dual list` shows grouped output by repo
   439→
   440→**Implementation Note**: After completing this phase and all automated verification passes, pause here for manual confirmation from the human that the manual testing was successful before proceeding to the next phase.

   443→
   444→## Phase 3: v3.0.0 — Architecture Rewrite (Scoping Only)
   445→
   446→### Overview
   447→This phase is scoped but NOT detailed for implementation yet. It defines what v3 contains so the v2.x work doesn't accidentally paint us into a corner.
   448→
   449→### 3.1 Multiplexer Trait
   450→
   451→Extract `src/tmux.rs` functions into a trait:
   452→```rust
   453→pub trait MultiplexerBackend {
   454→    fn create_session(&self, name: &str, cwd: &Path, init_cmd: Option<&str>) -> Result<()>;
   455→    fn attach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   456→    fn detach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   457→    fn destroy(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   458→    fn is_alive(&self, name: &str) -> bool;
   459→    fn list_sessions(&self) -> Vec<String>;
   460→    fn send_keys(&self, name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<()>;
   461→    fn session_name(&self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String;
   462→}
   463→```
   464→
   465→Implement `TmuxBackend`, stub `ZellijBackend`, add `BasicBackend` (no multiplexer).
   466→
   467→### 3.2 TUI
   468→
   469→Add ratatui + crossterm dependencies. New `src/tui/` module:
   470→- `mod.rs` — TUI app state and event loop
   471→- `tree.rs` — repo/branch tree view
   472→- `status.rs` — workspace status panel
   473→
   474→`dual` (no args) launches TUI. `dual list` becomes the non-interactive fallback.
   475→
   476→### 3.3 Session Layout Configuration
   477→
   478→Add to `.dual.toml`:
   479→```toml
   480→[layout]
   481→type = "editor-claude-shell"  # or custom
   482→
   483→[[layout.panes]]
   484→command = "nvim ."
   485→size = "60%"
   486→
   487→[[layout.panes]]
   488→command = "claude"
   489→size = "20%"
   490→
   491→[[layout.panes]]
   492→command = ""  # shell
   493→size = "20%"
   494→```
   495→
   496→### 3.4 Plugin System (Future)
   497→
   498→Design TBD. Likely TOML-declared hooks + shell commands:
   499→```toml
   500→[hooks]
   501→post_launch = "pnpm install && pnpm dev"
   502→pre_destroy = "pnpm build"
   503→```
   504→
   505→### What v3 Requires That v2.x Must NOT Break:
   506→- `config.rs` naming conventions must remain stable
   507→- `WorkspaceEntry` struct can add fields but not change existing ones
   508→- `container.rs` function signatures can evolve (already changing in v2.1)
   509→- `.dual.toml` format must remain backward-compatible (new fields with defaults)
   510→

   546→
   547→## Testing Strategy
   548→
   549→### Unit Tests:
   550→- Clone module: test local clone args, fallback behavior, checkout -b construction
   551→- Config module: test template generation, extra_commands/anonymous_volumes parsing
   552→- Container module: test env var args, anonymous volume args, setup exec args
   553→- Shell module: test merged command lists (defaults + extras)
   554→- Tmux module: test `$TMUX` detection, switch-client vs attach args
   555→- CLI module: test optional args parsing
   556→
   557→### Integration Tests:
   558→- Full launch pipeline with env vars → verify env inside container
   559→- Full launch pipeline with setup command → verify setup ran
   560→- Clone from local → verify new branch exists
   561→- Context detection → verify auto-detect from cwd
   562→
   563→### Manual Testing Steps:
   564→1. Create a workspace with `dual create myrepo feat/new-thing` where `feat/new-thing` doesn't exist at origin
   565→2. Verify `.dual.toml` has helpful comments when created by `dual add`
   566→3. Add env vars to `.dual.toml`, launch, verify inside container
   567→4. Set `setup = "echo hello > /tmp/setup-ran"`, launch, verify file exists
   568→5. Run `dual create feat/test` from inside a repo directory (no repo arg)
   569→6. Run `dual launch` from inside tmux — verify no nesting
   570→
   571→## Performance Considerations
   572→
   573→- Clone from local with `--local` uses hardlinks — significantly faster than remote clone
   574→- `detect_workspace()` and `detect_git_repo()` run git commands — acceptable for interactive CLI, but TUI (v3) should cache results
   575→- Anonymous volumes list is typically small (1-3 items) — no performance concern
   576→
   577→## Migration Notes
   578→
   579→### v2.0.0 → v2.1.0
   580→- **`.dual.toml` backward compatible**: New fields (`extra_commands`, `anonymous_volumes`) have defaults via `#[serde(default)]`
   581→- **Existing workspaces**: Already-cloned workspaces are unaffected. Only new `dual create` uses the new clone strategy
   582→- **Existing containers**: Must be recreated to pick up env vars/anonymous volumes. `dual destroy` + `dual launch` handles this
   583→
   584→### v2.1.0 → v2.2.0
   585→- **CLI breaking change**: `dual create` positional args change from `<repo> <branch>` to `<branch> [--repo REPO]`. This is a minor breaking change — document in changelog
   586→- **Optional workspace args**: Previously required args become optional. Fully backward compatible (explicit args still work)
   587→
   588→### v2.2.0 → v3.0.0
   589→- **TUI as default**: `dual` (no args) changes from showing list to launching TUI. `dual list` remains as non-interactive fallback
   590→- **Multiplexer trait**: Internal refactor, no user-facing change unless using zellij
   591→

```
     1→use std::path::PathBuf;

     3→use clap::Parser;

     5→use dual::clone;

    10→use dual::shell;
    11→use dual::state;
    12→use dual::tmux;
    13→use tracing::{debug, error, info, warn};
    14→
    15→fn main() {
    16→    // Initialize tracing with DUAL_LOG env var (default: info)
    17→    tracing_subscriber::fmt()
    18→        .with_env_filter(
    19→            tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::try_from_env("DUAL_LOG")
    20→                .unwrap_or_else(|_| tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter::new("info")),
    21→        )
    22→        .without_time()
    23→        .with_target(false)
    24→        .init();
    25→
    26→    let cli = Cli::parse();
    27→
    28→    let exit_code = match cli.command {
    29→        None => cmd_default(),
    30→        Some(Command::Add { name }) => cmd_add(name.as_deref()),
    31→        Some(Command::Create { repo, branch }) => cmd_create(&repo, &branch),
    32→        Some(Command::Launch { workspace }) => cmd_launch(&workspace),
    33→        Some(Command::List) => cmd_list(),
    34→        Some(Command::Destroy { workspace }) => cmd_destroy(&workspace),
    35→        Some(Command::Open { workspace }) => cmd_open(workspace),
    36→        Some(Command::Urls { workspace }) => cmd_urls(workspace),
    37→        Some(Command::Sync { workspace }) => cmd_sync(workspace),
    38→        Some(Command::Proxy) => cmd_proxy(),
    39→        Some(Command::ShellRc { container }) => cmd_shell_rc(&container),
    40→    };
    41→
    42→    std::process::exit(exit_code);
    43→}

    45→/// Default (no subcommand): show workspace list with launch hint.
    46→fn cmd_default() -> i32 {

    48→        Ok(s) => s,

    50→            error!("{e}");
    51→            info!("Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");

    53→        }
    54→    };
    55→
    56→    let workspaces = st.all_workspaces();

    58→        info!("No workspaces. Run `dual add` inside a repo to get started.");
    59→        return 0;
    60→    }
    61→
    62→    info!("Workspaces:\n");
    63→    print_workspace_status(&st);
    64→    info!("Use `dual launch <workspace>` to start a workspace.");
    65→    info!("Use `dual add` to register a new repo.");

    69→/// Register the current repo as a dual workspace.
    70→fn cmd_add(name: Option<&str>) -> i32 {
    71→    // Detect git repo info from current directory
    72→    let (repo_root, url, branch) = match detect_git_repo() {
    73→        Ok(info) => info,
    74→        Err(e) => {
    75→            error!("{e}");
    76→            return 1;
    77→        }
    78→    };
    79→
    80→    // Derive repo name
    81→    let repo_name = match name {
    82→        Some(n) => n.to_string(),
    83→        None => derive_repo_name(&repo_root),
    84→    };
    85→
    86→    // Load or create state
    87→    let mut st = match state::load() {
    88→        Ok(s) => s,
    89→        Err(e) => {
    90→            error!("{e}");
    91→            return 1;
    92→        }
    93→    };
    94→
    95→    // Check for duplicates
    96→    if st.has_workspace(&repo_name, &branch) {
    97→        error!("workspace {}/{} already exists", repo_name, branch);
    98→        return 1;
    99→    }
   100→
   101→    // Check for .dual.toml — if missing, create a default one with helpful comments
   102→    let hints_path = repo_root.join(".dual.toml");
   103→    if !hints_path.exists() {
   104→        if let Err(e) = config::write_default_hints(&repo_root) {
   105→            warn!("failed to write .dual.toml: {e}");
   106→        } else {
   107→            info!("Created .dual.toml with defaults (image: node:20)");
   108→            info!("Edit it to customize ports, image, setup command, and env vars.");
   109→        }
   110→    }
   111→
   112→    // Initialize shared directory if [shared] is configured
   113→    let hints = config::load_hints(&repo_root).unwrap_or_default();
   114→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   115→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   116→    {
   117→        match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&repo_name) {
   118→            Ok(shared_dir) => {
   119→                match shared::init_from_main(&repo_root, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   120→                    Ok(moved) => {
   121→                        for f in &moved {
   122→                            info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{repo_name}/");
   123→                        }
   124→                    }
   125→                    Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   126→                }
   127→            }
   128→            Err(e) => warn!("could not create shared directory: {e}"),
   129→        }
   130→    }
   131→
   132→    // Add workspace entry
   133→    let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
   134→        repo: repo_name.clone(),
   135→        url,
   136→        branch: branch.clone(),
   137→        path: Some(repo_root.to_string_lossy().to_string()),
   138→    };
   139→
   140→    if let Err(e) = st.add_workspace(entry) {
   141→        error!("{e}");
   142→        return 1;
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    // Save state
   146→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   147→        error!("failed to save state: {e}");
   148→        return 1;
   149→    }
   150→
   151→    let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&repo_name, &branch);
   152→    info!("Added workspace: {ws_id}");
   153→    info!("Use `dual launch {ws_id}` to start.");
   154→    0
   155→}
   156→
   157→/// Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo.
   158→fn cmd_create(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> i32 {
   159→    let mut st = match state::load() {
   160→        Ok(s) => s,
   161→        Err(e) => {
   162→            error!("{e}");
   163→            return 1;
   164→        }
   165→    };
   166→
   167→    // Find an existing workspace for this repo
   168→    let existing = st.workspaces_for_repo(repo);
   169→    if existing.is_empty() {
   170→        error!("repo '{repo}' not found. Run `dual add` inside the repo first.");
   171→        return 1;
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    // Check if this branch already exists
   175→    if st.has_workspace(repo, branch) {
   176→        error!("workspace {repo}/{branch} already exists");
   177→        return 1;
   178→    }
   179→
   180→    // Get URL from existing entry
   181→    let url = existing[0].url.clone();
   182→
   183→    // Add new entry (no explicit path — will be cloned on launch)
   184→    let entry = state::WorkspaceEntry {
   185→        repo: repo.to_string(),
   186→        url,
   187→        branch: branch.to_string(),
   188→        path: None,
   189→    };
   190→
   191→    if let Err(e) = st.add_workspace(entry) {
   192→        error!("{e}");
   193→        return 1;
   194→    }
   195→
   196→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   197→        error!("failed to save state: {e}");
   198→        return 1;
   199→    }
   200→
   201→    let ws_id = config::workspace_id(repo, branch);
   202→    info!("Created workspace: {ws_id}");
   203→    info!("Use `dual launch {ws_id}` to start.");
   204→    0
   205→}
   206→
   207→/// Launch a specific workspace: clone → container → shell RC → tmux → attach.
   208→fn cmd_launch(workspace: &str) -> i32 {
   209→    let st = match state::load() {
   210→        Ok(s) => s,
   211→        Err(e) => {
   212→            error!("{e}");
   213→            return 1;
   214→        }
   215→    };
   216→
   217→    let entry = match st.resolve_workspace(workspace) {
   218→        Some(e) => e,
   219→        None => {
   220→            error!("unknown workspace '{workspace}'");
   221→            info!("Configured workspaces:");
   222→            for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   223→                let id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   224→                info!("  {id}");
   225→            }
   226→            return 1;
   227→        }
   228→    };
   229→
   230→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   231→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   232→    let session_name = tmux::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   233→    debug!(
   234→        repo = %entry.repo,
   235→        branch = %entry.branch,
   236→        "resolved workspace"
   237→    );
   238→
   239→    // Step 1: Resolve workspace directory
   240→    let workspace_dir = if let Some(ref path) = entry.path {
   241→        let dir = PathBuf::from(path);
   242→        if !dir.join(".git").exists() {
   243→            error!(
   244→                "workspace path {} does not contain a git repo",
   245→                dir.display()
   246→            );
   247→            return 1;
   248→        }
   249→        dir
   250→    } else {
   251→        // Try to clone from local main workspace first (fast, hardlinks)
   252→        let target_dir = config::workspace_dir(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   253→        let main_workspace_path = st
   254→            .workspaces_for_repo(&entry.repo)
   255→            .into_iter()
   256→            .find(|ws| ws.path.is_some())
   257→            .and_then(|ws| ws.path.as_ref().map(PathBuf::from));
   258→
   259→        match main_workspace_path {
   260→            Some(main_path) if main_path.join(".git").exists() => {
   261→                info!("Cloning from local main workspace...");
   262→                match clone::clone_from_local(&main_path, &target_dir, &entry.branch) {
   263→                    Ok(dir) => dir,
   264→                    Err(e) => {
   265→                        error!("local clone failed: {e}");
   266→                        return 1;
   267→                    }
   268→                }
   269→            }
   270→            _ => {
   271→                // Fallback: clone from remote URL
   272→                match clone::clone_workspace(
   273→                    &workspace_root,
   274→                    &entry.repo,
   275→                    &entry.url,
   276→                    &entry.branch,
   277→                ) {
   278→                    Ok(dir) => dir,
   279→                    Err(e) => {
   280→                        error!("clone failed: {e}");
   281→                        return 1;
   282→                    }
   283→                }
   284→            }
   285→        }
   286→    };
   287→
   288→    // Step 2: Handle shared files
   289→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   290→    if let Some(ref shared_config) = hints.shared
   291→        && !shared_config.files.is_empty()
   292→        && let Ok(shared_dir) = shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo)
   293→    {
   294→        if entry.path.is_some() {
   295→            // Main workspace: ensure shared files are initialized
   296→            match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   297→                Ok(moved) => {
   298→                    for f in &moved {
   299→                        info!("  shared: {f} → ~/.dual/shared/{}/", entry.repo);
   300→                    }
   301→                }
   302→                Err(e) => warn!("shared init failed: {e}"),
   303→            }
   304→        } else {
   305→            // Branch workspace: copy shared files
   306→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   307→                Ok(copied) => {
   308→                    for f in &copied {
   309→                        info!("  shared: copied {f}");
   310→                    }
   311→                }
   312→                Err(e) => warn!("shared copy failed: {e}"),
   313→            }
   314→        }
   315→    }
   316→
   317→    // Step 3: Ensure container exists and is running
   318→    let is_new_container = matches!(
   319→        container::status(&container_name),
   320→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing
   321→    );
   322→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   323→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {
   324→            info!("Creating container {container_name}...");
   325→            if let Err(e) = container::create(
   326→                &container_name,
   327→                &workspace_dir,
   328→                &hints.image,
   329→                &hints.env,
   330→                &hints.anonymous_volumes,
   331→            ) {
   332→                error!("container create failed: {e}");
   333→                return 1;
   334→            }
   335→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   336→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   337→                return 1;
   338→            }
   339→        }
   340→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   341→            info!("Starting container {container_name}...");
   342→            if let Err(e) = container::start(&container_name) {
   343→                error!("container start failed: {e}");
   344→                return 1;
   345→            }
   346→        }
   347→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {}
   348→    }
   349→
   350→    // Step 3.5: Run setup command on new containers
   351→    if is_new_container && let Some(ref setup) = hints.setup {
   352→        info!("Running setup: {setup}");
   353→        if let Err(e) = container::exec_setup(&container_name, setup) {
   354→            error!("setup failed: {e}");
   355→            return 1;
   356→        }
   357→    }
   358→
   359→    // Step 4: Write shell RC file
   360→    let rc_path = match shell::write_rc_file(&container_name, &hints.extra_commands) {
   361→        Ok(p) => p,
   362→        Err(e) => {
   363→            error!("failed to write shell RC: {e}");
   364→            return 1;
   365→        }
   366→    };
   367→
   368→    // Step 5: Create tmux session if not alive
   369→    if !tmux::is_alive(&session_name) {
   370→        let source_cmd = shell::source_file_command(&rc_path);
   371→        if let Err(e) = tmux::create_session(&session_name, &workspace_dir, Some(&source_cmd)) {
   372→            error!("tmux session creation failed: {e}");
   373→            return 1;
   374→        }
   375→    }
   376→
   377→    // Step 6: Attach
   378→    info!("Attaching to {session_name}...");
   379→    if let Err(e) = tmux::attach(&session_name) {
   380→        error!("tmux attach failed: {e}");
   381→        return 1;
   382→    }
   383→
   384→    0
   385→}
   386→
   387→/// List all configured workspaces with their live status.
   388→fn cmd_list() -> i32 {
   389→    let st = match state::load() {
   390→        Ok(s) => s,
   391→        Err(e) => {
   392→            error!("{e}");
   393→            return 1;
   394→        }
   395→    };
   396→
   397→    let workspaces = st.all_workspaces();
   398→    if workspaces.is_empty() {
   399→        info!("No workspaces configured.");
   400→        return 0;
   401→    }
   402→
   403→    print_workspace_status(&st);
   404→    0
   405→}
   406→
   407→/// Destroy a workspace: tmux → container → clone.
   408→fn cmd_destroy(workspace: &str) -> i32 {
   409→    let mut st = match state::load() {
   410→        Ok(s) => s,
   411→        Err(e) => {
   412→            error!("{e}");
   413→            return 1;
   414→        }
   415→    };
   416→
   417→    let entry = match st.resolve_workspace(workspace) {
   418→        Some(e) => e.clone(),
   419→        None => {
   420→            error!("unknown workspace '{workspace}'");
   421→            return 1;
   422→        }
   423→    };
   424→
   425→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   426→    let container_name = config::container_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   427→    let session_name = tmux::session_name(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   428→
   429→    // Destroy tmux session
   430→    if tmux::is_alive(&session_name) {
   431→        info!("Destroying tmux session {session_name}...");
   432→        if let Err(e) = tmux::destroy(&session_name) {
   433→            warn!("tmux destroy failed: {e}");
   434→        }
   435→    }
   436→
   437→    // Stop and remove container
   438→    match container::status(&container_name) {
   439→        container::ContainerStatus::Running => {
   440→            info!("Stopping container {container_name}...");
   441→            if let Err(e) = container::stop(&container_name) {
   442→                warn!("container stop failed: {e}");
   443→            }
   444→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   445→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   446→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   447→            }
   448→        }
   449→        container::ContainerStatus::Stopped => {
   450→            info!("Removing container {container_name}...");
   451→            if let Err(e) = container::destroy(&container_name) {
   452→                warn!("container remove failed: {e}");
   453→            }
   454→        }
   455→        container::ContainerStatus::Missing => {}
   456→    }
   457→
   458→    // Remove clone (only for non-explicit-path workspaces)
   459→    if entry.path.is_none() && clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch)
   460→    {
   461→        info!("Removing clone...");
   462→        if let Err(e) = clone::remove_workspace(&workspace_root, &entry.repo, &entry.branch) {
   463→            error!("failed to remove clone: {e}");
   464→            return 1;
   465→        }
   466→    }
   467→
   468→    // Remove from state
   469→    st.remove_workspace(&entry.repo, &entry.branch);
   470→    if let Err(e) = state::save(&st) {
   471→        warn!("failed to save state: {e}");
   472→    }
   473→
   474→    info!("Workspace '{workspace}' destroyed.");
   475→    0
   476→}
   477→
   478→/// Open workspace services in the default browser.
   479→fn cmd_open(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   480→    let st = match state::load() {
   481→        Ok(s) => s,
   482→        Err(e) => {
   483→            error!("{e}");
   484→            return 1;
   485→        }
   486→    };
   487→
   488→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   489→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   490→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   491→        return 0;
   492→    }
   493→
   494→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   495→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   496→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   497→        None => url_groups,
   498→    };
   499→
   500→    if filtered.is_empty() {
   501→        if let Some(ws) = &workspace {
   502→            error!("no URLs for workspace '{ws}'");
   503→        }
   504→        return 1;
   505→    }
   506→
   507→    for (_, urls) in &filtered {
   508→        for url_line in urls {
   509→            // Extract the URL part (after the status icon)
   510→            let url = url_line
   511→                .trim()
   512→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cf}')
   513→                .trim_start_matches('\u{25cb}')
   514→                .trim();
   515→            let http_url = format!("http://{url}");
   516→            #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
   517→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("open").arg(&http_url).spawn();
   518→            #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
   519→            let _ = std::process::Command::new("xdg-open")
   520→                .arg(&http_url)
   521→                .spawn();
   522→            info!("Opening {http_url}");
   523→        }
   524→    }
   525→
   526→    0
   527→}
   528→
   529→/// Show workspace URLs.
   530→fn cmd_urls(workspace: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   531→    let st = match state::load() {
   532→        Ok(s) => s,
   533→        Err(e) => {
   534→            error!("{e}");
   535→            return 1;
   536→        }
   537→    };
   538→
   539→    let url_groups = proxy::workspace_urls(&st);
   540→    if url_groups.is_empty() {
   541→        info!("No URLs configured. Add 'ports' to .dual.toml in your repo.");
   542→        return 0;
   543→    }
   544→
   545→    // Filter by workspace if specified
   546→    let filtered: Vec<_> = match &workspace {
   547→        Some(ws) => url_groups.into_iter().filter(|(id, _)| id == ws).collect(),
   548→        None => url_groups,
   549→    };
   550→
   551→    for (workspace_id, urls) in &filtered {
   552→        info!("{workspace_id}");

   562→/// Start the reverse proxy.
   563→fn cmd_proxy() -> i32 {
   564→    let st = match state::load() {
   565→        Ok(s) => s,
   566→        Err(e) => {
   567→            error!("{e}");
   568→            return 1;
   569→        }
   570→    };
   571→
   572→    let rt = tokio::runtime::Runtime::new().expect("failed to create tokio runtime");
   573→    match rt.block_on(proxy::start(&st)) {
   574→        Ok(()) => 0,
   575→        Err(e) => {
   576→            error!("proxy failed: {e}");
   577→            1
   578→        }
   579→    }
   580→}

   587→
   588→/// Sync shared config files for a workspace.
   589→fn cmd_sync(workspace_arg: Option<String>) -> i32 {
   590→    let st = match state::load() {
   591→        Ok(s) => s,
   592→        Err(e) => {
   593→            error!("{e}");
   594→            return 1;
   595→        }
   596→    };
   597→
   598→    // Resolve which workspace we're syncing
   599→    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {
   600→        match st.resolve_workspace(&ws) {
   601→            Some(e) => e.clone(),
   602→            None => {
   603→                error!("unknown workspace '{ws}'");
   604→                return 1;
   605→            }
   606→        }
   607→    } else {
   608→        match detect_workspace(&st) {
   609→            Some(e) => e,
   610→            None => {
   611→                error!("not inside a dual workspace");
   612→                info!("Usage: dual sync [workspace]");
   613→                return 1;
   614→            }
   615→        }
   616→    };
   617→
   618→    // Load hints
   619→    let workspace_dir = st.workspace_dir(&entry);
   620→    let hints = config::load_hints(&workspace_dir).unwrap_or_default();
   621→    let shared_config = match &hints.shared {
   622→        Some(s) if !s.files.is_empty() => s,
   623→        _ => {
   624→            error!("no [shared] section in .dual.toml (or files list is empty)");
   625→            return 1;
   626→        }
   627→    };
   628→
   629→    let shared_dir = match shared::ensure_shared_dir(&entry.repo) {
   630→        Ok(d) => d,
   631→        Err(e) => {
   632→            error!("{e}");
   633→            return 1;
   634→        }
   635→    };
   636→
   637→    let is_main = entry.path.is_some();
   638→
   639→    if is_main {
   640→        // Main workspace: init shared dir, then prompt to sync all branches
   641→        match shared::init_from_main(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   642→            Ok(moved) => {
   643→                for f in &moved {
   644→                    info!("  moved {f} → shared/");
   645→                }
   646→            }
   647→            Err(e) => {
   648→                error!("{e}");
   649→                return 1;
   650→            }
   651→        }
   652→
   653→        // Prompt to sync all branches
   654→        let branches: Vec<_> = st
   655→            .workspaces_for_repo(&entry.repo)
   656→            .into_iter()
   657→            .filter(|ws| ws.path.is_none())
   658→            .collect();
   659→
   660→        if branches.is_empty() {
   661→            info!("No branch workspaces to sync.");
   662→            return 0;
   663→        }
   664→
   665→        // Interactive prompt — use println! directly since this is user interaction
   666→        println!(
   667→            "\nSync shared files to ALL {} branch workspace(s)? [y/N]",
   668→            branches.len()
   669→        );
   670→        let mut input = String::new();
   671→        std::io::stdin().read_line(&mut input).unwrap_or(0);
   672→        if !input.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("y") {
   673→            info!("Cancelled.");
   674→            return 0;
   675→        }
   676→
   677→        for branch_entry in &branches {
   678→            let branch_dir = st.workspace_dir(branch_entry);
   679→            if !branch_dir.exists() {
   680→                continue; // Not yet cloned
   681→            }
   682→            let ws_id = config::workspace_id(&branch_entry.repo, &branch_entry.branch);
   683→            match shared::copy_to_branch(&branch_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   684→                Ok(copied) => {
   685→                    info!("{ws_id}: synced {} file(s)", copied.len());
   686→                }
   687→                Err(e) => error!("{ws_id}: {e}"),
   688→            }
   689→        }
   690→    } else {
   691→        // Branch workspace: copy from shared dir
   692→        match shared::copy_to_branch(&workspace_dir, &shared_dir, &shared_config.files) {
   693→            Ok(copied) => {
   694→                if copied.is_empty() {
   695→                    info!(
   696→                        "No shared files available yet. Run `dual sync` in the main workspace first."
   697→                    );
   698→                } else {
   699→                    for f in &copied {
   700→                        info!("  synced {f}");
   701→                    }
   702→                }
   703→            }
   704→            Err(e) => {
   705→                error!("{e}");
   706→                return 1;
   707→            }
   708→        }
   709→    }
   710→
   711→    0
   712→}
   713→
   714→/// Detect which workspace the current directory belongs to.
   715→fn detect_workspace(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<state::WorkspaceEntry> {
   716→    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;
   717→
   718→    // Try git root first (handles being in subdirectories)
   719→    let root = std::process::Command::new("git")
   720→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   721→        .output()
   722→        .ok()
   723→        .filter(|o| o.status.success())
   724→        .map(|o| PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()))
   725→        .unwrap_or(cwd);
   726→
   727→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   728→        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);
   729→        if ws_dir == root {
   730→            return Some(ws.clone());
   731→        }
   732→    }
   733→    None
   734→}
   735→
   736→/// Print workspace status table.
   737→fn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState) {
   738→    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();
   739→    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {
   740→        let workspace_id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   741→        let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   742→        let session_name = tmux::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
   743→
   744→        let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {
   745→            // Explicit path — check if the path exists
   746→            ws.path
   747→                .as_ref()
   748→                .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(".git").exists())
   749→                .unwrap_or(false)
   750→        } else {
   751→            clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)
   752→        };
   753→        let container_st = container::status(&container_name);
   754→        let tmux_alive = tmux::is_alive(&session_name);
   755→
   756→        let status_icon = match (&container_st, tmux_alive) {
   757→            (container::ContainerStatus::Running, true) => "\u{25cf} attached",
   758→            (container::ContainerStatus::Running, false) => "\u{25cf} running",
   759→            (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, _) => "\u{25cb} stopped",
   760→            (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) if clone_exists => "\u{25cb} stopped",
   761→            (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) => "\u{25cc} lazy",
   762→        };
   763→
   764→        info!("  {workspace_id:<30} {status_icon}");
   765→    }
   766→}
   767→
   768→/// Detect git repo info from the current directory.
   769→fn detect_git_repo() -> Result<(PathBuf, String, String), String> {
   770→    // Get repo root
   771→    let root_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   772→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   773→        .output()
   774→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   775→    if !root_output.status.success() {
   776→        return Err("not inside a git repository".to_string());
   777→    }
   778→    let root = PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&root_output.stdout).trim());
   779→
   780→    // Get remote URL
   781→    let url_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   782→        .args(["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
   783→        .output()
   784→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   785→    let url = if url_output.status.success() {
   786→        String::from_utf8_lossy(&url_output.stdout)
   787→            .trim()
   788→            .to_string()
   789→    } else {
   790→        root.to_string_lossy().to_string() // local-only repo, use path as URL
   791→    };
   792→
   793→    // Get current branch
   794→    let branch_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   795→        .args(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"])
   796→        .output()
   797→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   798→    let branch = String::from_utf8_lossy(&branch_output.stdout)
   799→        .trim()
   800→        .to_string();
   801→
   802→    Ok((root, url, branch))
   803→}
   804→
   805→/// Derive a short repo name from a directory path.
   806→/// "/Users/jeevan/code/lightfast" → "lightfast"
   807→fn derive_repo_name(path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
   808→    path.file_name()
   809→        .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
   810→        .unwrap_or("repo")
   811→        .to_string()
   812→}
```

```
     1→use std::path::Path;
     2→use std::process::Command;
     3→
     4→/// Session name prefix for dual-managed sessions.
     5→const SESSION_PREFIX: &str = "dual-";
     6→
     7→/// Check if tmux is available on the system.
     8→pub fn is_available() -> bool {
     9→    Command::new("tmux")
    10→        .arg("-V")
    11→        .output()
    12→        .is_ok_and(|o| o.status.success())
    13→}
    14→
    15→/// Create a new detached tmux session for a workspace.
    16→///
    17→/// - Session name follows the container naming convention: dual-{repo}-{branch}
    18→/// - Working directory is set to the workspace clone dir
    19→/// - Optionally sources shell RC for command interception
    20→pub fn create_session(
    21→    session_name: &str,
    22→    cwd: &Path,
    23→    shell_rc: Option<&str>,
    24→) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    25→    // Create detached session with CWD
    26→    let output = Command::new("tmux")
    27→        .args(build_new_session_args(session_name, cwd))
    28→        .output()
    29→        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;
    30→
    31→    if !output.status.success() {
    32→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
    33→        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {
    34→            operation: "new-session".to_string(),
    35→            session: session_name.to_string(),
    36→            stderr,
    37→        });
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    // Source shell RC if provided
    41→    if let Some(rc) = shell_rc {
    42→        send_keys(session_name, rc)?;
    43→    }
    44→
    45→    Ok(())
    46→}
    47→
    48→/// Attach to an existing tmux session.
    49→pub fn attach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    50→    let status = Command::new("tmux")
    51→        .args(["attach-session", "-t", session_name])
    52→        .status()
    53→        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;
    54→
    55→    if !status.success() {
    56→        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {
    57→            operation: "attach-session".to_string(),
    58→            session: session_name.to_string(),
    59→            stderr: format!("exit code: {}", status.code().unwrap_or(-1)),
    60→        });
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    Ok(())
    64→}
    65→
    66→/// Detach the current client from a session.
    67→pub fn detach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    68→    tmux_simple(&["detach-client", "-s", session_name])
    69→}
    70→
    71→/// Destroy a tmux session (kill all panes and processes).
    72→pub fn destroy(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
    73→    tmux_simple(&["kill-session", "-t", session_name])
    74→}
    75→
    76→/// Check if a session exists and is alive.
    77→pub fn is_alive(session_name: &str) -> bool {
    78→    Command::new("tmux")
    79→        .args(["has-session", "-t", session_name])
    80→        .output()
    81→        .is_ok_and(|o| o.status.success())
    82→}
    83→
    84→/// List all dual-managed tmux sessions.
    85→/// Returns session names that start with the dual- prefix.
    86→pub fn list_sessions() -> Vec<String> {
    87→    let output = Command::new("tmux")
    88→        .args(["list-sessions", "-F", "#{session_name}"])
    89→        .output();
    90→
    91→    match output {
    92→        Ok(out) if out.status.success() => {
    93→            let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    94→            stdout
    95→                .lines()
    96→                .filter(|line| line.starts_with(SESSION_PREFIX))
    97→                .map(|s| s.to_string())
    98→                .collect()
    99→        }
   100→        _ => Vec::new(),
   101→    }
   102→}
   103→
   104→/// Send keys (a command string) to a tmux session pane.
   105→pub fn send_keys(session_name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
   106→    tmux_simple(&["send-keys", "-t", session_name, keys, "Enter"])
   107→}
   108→
   109→/// Build the arguments for tmux new-session (for testing).
   110→pub fn build_new_session_args(session_name: &str, cwd: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
   111→    vec![
   112→        "new-session".to_string(),
   113→        "-d".to_string(),
   114→        "-s".to_string(),
   115→        session_name.to_string(),
   116→        "-c".to_string(),
   117→        cwd.to_string_lossy().to_string(),
   118→    ]
   119→}
   120→
   121→/// Generate the session name for a workspace.
   122→pub fn session_name(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String {
   123→    use crate::config::encode_branch;
   124→    format!("{SESSION_PREFIX}{repo}-{}", encode_branch(branch))
   125→}
   126→
   127→fn tmux_simple(args: &[&str]) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {
   128→    let output = Command::new("tmux")
   129→        .args(args)
   130→        .output()
   131→        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;
   132→
   133→    if !output.status.success() {
   134→        let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).to_string();
   135→        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {
   136→            operation: args.first().unwrap_or(&"unknown").to_string(),
   137→            session: args
   138→                .iter()
   139→                .position(|&a| a == "-t" || a == "-s")
   140→                .and_then(|i| args.get(i + 1))
   141→                .unwrap_or(&"unknown")
   142→                .to_string(),
   143→            stderr,
   144→        });
   145→    }
   146→
   147→    Ok(())
   148→}
   149→
   150→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   151→pub enum TmuxError {
   152→    #[error("tmux not found: {0}")]
   153→    NotFound(String),
   154→
   155→    #[error("tmux {operation} failed for {session}: {stderr}")]
   156→    Failed {
   157→        operation: String,
   158→        session: String,
   159→        stderr: String,
   160→    },
   161→}
   162→

   169→    fn new_session_args_correct() {
   170→        let args = build_new_session_args(

   173→        );
   174→        assert_eq!(
```

```
     1→use fs2::FileExt;
     2→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
     3→use std::fs::{self, File};

     5→use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     6→use tracing::debug;
     7→
     8→use crate::config;
     9→
    10→const STATE_DIR: &str = ".dual";
    11→const STATE_FILENAME: &str = "workspaces.toml";
    12→const DEFAULT_WORKSPACE_ROOT: &str = ".dual/workspaces";
    13→
    14→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    15→pub struct WorkspaceState {
    16→    /// Root directory for all workspace clones (default: ~/.dual/workspaces)
    17→    pub workspace_root: Option<String>,
    18→
    19→    /// Active workspace entries
    20→    #[serde(default)]
    21→    pub workspaces: Vec<WorkspaceEntry>,
    22→}
    23→
    24→#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Clone)]
    25→pub struct WorkspaceEntry {
    26→    /// Short repo name (e.g. "lightfast")
    27→    pub repo: String,
    28→
    29→    /// Git URL or local path
    30→    pub url: String,
    31→
    32→    /// Branch name (e.g. "main", "feat/auth")
    33→    pub branch: String,
    34→
    35→    /// Explicit path to workspace directory (for `dual add` — user's existing clone).
    36→    /// If None, workspace lives at {workspace_root}/{repo}/{encoded_branch}/
    37→    /// and will be cloned on first launch.
    38→    pub path: Option<String>,
    39→}
    40→
    41→impl WorkspaceState {
    42→    /// Create a new empty state.
    43→    pub fn new() -> Self {
    44→        Self {
    45→            workspace_root: None,
    46→            workspaces: Vec::new(),
    47→        }
    48→    }
    49→
    50→    /// Resolve the workspace root directory as an absolute path.
    51→    /// Uses the configured value or defaults to ~/.dual/workspaces.
    52→    pub fn workspace_root(&self) -> PathBuf {
    53→        if let Some(ref root) = self.workspace_root {
    54→            let expanded = shellexpand(root);
    55→            PathBuf::from(expanded)
    56→        } else {
    57→            dirs::home_dir()
    58→                .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("."))
    59→                .join(DEFAULT_WORKSPACE_ROOT)
    60→        }
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    /// Get the workspace directory for an entry.
    64→    /// Uses explicit path if set, otherwise computes from workspace_root.
    65→    pub fn workspace_dir(&self, entry: &WorkspaceEntry) -> PathBuf {
    66→        if let Some(ref path) = entry.path {
    67→            PathBuf::from(shellexpand(path))
    68→        } else {
    69→            config::workspace_dir(&self.workspace_root(), &entry.repo, &entry.branch)
    70→        }
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    /// Find a workspace entry by identifier (e.g. "lightfast-main").
    74→    pub fn resolve_workspace(&self, identifier: &str) -> Option<&WorkspaceEntry> {
    75→        self.workspaces.iter().find(|ws| {
    76→            let id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);
    77→            id == identifier
    78→        })
    79→    }
    80→
    81→    /// Get all workspace entries.
    82→    pub fn all_workspaces(&self) -> &[WorkspaceEntry] {
    83→        &self.workspaces
    84→    }
    85→
    86→    /// Add a workspace entry. Returns Err if duplicate exists.
    87→    pub fn add_workspace(&mut self, entry: WorkspaceEntry) -> Result<(), StateError> {
    88→        if self.has_workspace(&entry.repo, &entry.branch) {
    89→            return Err(StateError::DuplicateWorkspace(
    90→                entry.repo.clone(),
    91→                entry.branch.clone(),
    92→            ));
    93→        }
    94→        self.workspaces.push(entry);
    95→        Ok(())
    96→    }
    97→
    98→    /// Remove a workspace entry by repo + branch. Returns true if found.
    99→    pub fn remove_workspace(&mut self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> bool {
   100→        let before = self.workspaces.len();
   101→        self.workspaces
   102→            .retain(|ws| !(ws.repo == repo && ws.branch == branch));
   103→        self.workspaces.len() < before
   104→    }
   105→
   106→    /// Find all workspaces for a given repo name.
   107→    pub fn workspaces_for_repo(&self, repo: &str) -> Vec<&WorkspaceEntry> {
   108→        self.workspaces
   109→            .iter()
   110→            .filter(|ws| ws.repo == repo)
   111→            .collect()
   112→    }
   113→
   114→    /// Check if a workspace entry exists for repo + branch.
   115→    pub fn has_workspace(&self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> bool {
   116→        self.workspaces
   117→            .iter()
   118→            .any(|ws| ws.repo == repo && ws.branch == branch)
   119→    }
   120→}
   121→
   122→impl Default for WorkspaceState {
   123→    fn default() -> Self {
   124→        Self::new()
   125→    }
   126→}
   127→
   128→/// Get the state file path: ~/.dual/workspaces.toml
   129→pub fn state_path() -> Option<PathBuf> {
   130→    dirs::home_dir().map(|home| home.join(STATE_DIR).join(STATE_FILENAME))
   131→}
   132→
   133→/// Load state from default location. Returns empty state if file doesn't exist.
   134→pub fn load() -> Result<WorkspaceState, StateError> {
   135→    let path = state_path().ok_or(StateError::NoHomeDir)?;
   136→
   137→    if !path.exists() {
   138→        return Ok(WorkspaceState::new());
   139→    }
   140→
   141→    let contents =
   142→        std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| StateError::ReadError(path.clone(), e))?;
   143→    let state: WorkspaceState =
   144→        toml::from_str(&contents).map_err(|e| StateError::ParseError(path.clone(), e))?;
   145→    validate(&state)?;
   146→    debug!(count = state.workspaces.len(), "loaded state");
   147→    Ok(state)
   148→}
   149→
   150→/// Load state from a specific path.
   151→pub fn load_from(path: &Path) -> Result<WorkspaceState, StateError> {
   152→    if !path.exists() {
   153→        return Ok(WorkspaceState::new());
   154→    }
   155→
   156→    let contents =
   157→        std::fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|e| StateError::ReadError(path.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   158→    let state: WorkspaceState =
   159→        toml::from_str(&contents).map_err(|e| StateError::ParseError(path.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   160→    validate(&state)?;
   161→    Ok(state)
   162→}
   163→
   164→/// Save state to default location. Uses atomic write with backup.
   165→pub fn save(state: &WorkspaceState) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   166→    let path = state_path().ok_or(StateError::NoHomeDir)?;
   167→
   168→    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
   169→        fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(parent.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   170→    }
   171→
   172→    atomic_save(state, &path)
   173→}
   174→
   175→/// Save state to a specific path. Uses atomic write with backup.
   176→pub fn save_to(state: &WorkspaceState, path: &Path) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   177→    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
   178→        fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(parent.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   179→    }
   180→
   181→    atomic_save(state, path)
   182→}
   183→
   184→/// Atomic save: serialize -> write to temp file -> backup existing -> rename.
   185→///
   186→/// Uses advisory file locking to prevent concurrent writes.
   187→/// The lock is held on a lockfile for the duration of the write-backup-rename sequence.
   188→fn atomic_save(state: &WorkspaceState, path: &Path) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   189→    let contents = toml::to_string_pretty(state).map_err(StateError::SerializeError)?;
   190→
   191→    let lock_path = path.with_extension("lock");
   192→
   193→    // Acquire advisory lock
   194→    let lock_file =
   195→        File::create(&lock_path).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(lock_path.clone(), e))?;
   196→    lock_file
   197→        .lock_exclusive()
   198→        .map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(lock_path.clone(), e))?;
   199→
   200→    // Write to temp file in the same directory (same filesystem for rename)
   201→    let tmp_path = path.with_extension("tmp");
   202→    let mut tmp_file =
   203→        File::create(&tmp_path).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(tmp_path.clone(), e))?;
   204→    tmp_file
   205→        .write_all(contents.as_bytes())
   206→        .map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(tmp_path.clone(), e))?;
   207→    tmp_file
   208→        .sync_all()
   209→        .map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(tmp_path.clone(), e))?;
   210→
   211→    // Backup existing file (best-effort — don't fail if original doesn't exist)
   212→    let bak_path = path.with_extension("toml.bak");
   213→    if path.exists() {
   214→        let _ = fs::copy(path, &bak_path);
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    // Atomic rename (POSIX guarantees this is atomic on same filesystem)
   218→    fs::rename(&tmp_path, path).map_err(|e| StateError::WriteError(path.to_path_buf(), e))?;
   219→
   220→    // Release lock (dropped with file, but explicit for clarity)
   221→    let _ = lock_file.unlock();
   222→    let _ = fs::remove_file(&lock_path);
   223→
   224→    Ok(())
   225→}
   226→
   227→/// Parse state from TOML string (for testing).
   228→pub fn parse(toml_str: &str) -> Result<WorkspaceState, StateError> {
   229→    let state: WorkspaceState = toml::from_str(toml_str)
   230→        .map_err(|e| StateError::ParseError(PathBuf::from("<string>"), e))?;
   231→    validate(&state)?;
   232→    Ok(state)
   233→}
   234→
   235→fn validate(state: &WorkspaceState) -> Result<(), StateError> {
   236→    for (i, ws) in state.workspaces.iter().enumerate() {
   237→        if ws.repo.is_empty() {
   238→            return Err(StateError::Validation(format!(
   239→                "workspaces[{i}]: 'repo' cannot be empty"
   240→            )));
   241→        }
   242→        if ws.url.is_empty() {
   243→            return Err(StateError::Validation(format!(
   244→                "workspaces[{i}] ({}): 'url' cannot be empty",
   245→                ws.repo
   246→            )));
   247→        }
   248→        if ws.branch.is_empty() {
   249→            return Err(StateError::Validation(format!(
   250→                "workspaces[{i}] ({}): 'branch' cannot be empty",
   251→                ws.repo
   252→            )));
   253→        }
   254→    }
   255→    Ok(())
   256→}
   257→
   258→/// Minimal ~ expansion for workspace_root paths.
   259→fn shellexpand(path: &str) -> String {
   260→    if let Some(rest) = path.strip_prefix("~/")
   261→        && let Some(home) = dirs::home_dir()
   262→    {
   263→        return home.join(rest).to_string_lossy().into_owned();
   264→    }
   265→    path.to_string()
   266→}
   267→
   268→#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
   269→pub enum StateError {
   270→    #[error("Could not determine home directory")]
   271→    NoHomeDir,
   272→
   273→    #[error("Failed to read {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   274→    ReadError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   275→
   276→    #[error("Failed to write {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   277→    WriteError(PathBuf, std::io::Error),
   278→
   279→    #[error("Failed to parse {path}: {err}", path = .0.display(), err = .1)]
   280→    ParseError(PathBuf, toml::de::Error),
   281→
   282→    #[error("Failed to serialize state: {0}")]
   283→    SerializeError(toml::ser::Error),
   284→
   285→    #[error("Invalid state: {0}")]
   286→    Validation(String),
   287→
   288→    #[error("Workspace {0}/{1} already exists")]
   289→    DuplicateWorkspace(String, String),
   290→}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo.\nfn cmd_create(repo: &str, branch: &str) -> i32 {\n    let mut st = match state::load() {\n        Ok(s) => s,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Find an existing workspace for this repo\n    let existing = st.workspaces_for_repo(repo);\n    if existing.is_empty() {\n        error!(\"repo '{repo}' not found. Run `dual add` inside the repo first.\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Check if this branch already exists\n    if st.has_workspace(repo, branch) {\n        error!(\"workspace {repo}/{branch} already exists\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Get URL from existing entry\n    let url = existing[0].url.clone();",
  "new_string": "/// Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo.\nfn cmd_create(repo_arg: Option<&str>, branch: &str) -> i32 {\n    let mut st = match state::load() {\n        Ok(s) => s,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Resolve repo name: use --repo if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd\n    let repo = match repo_arg {\n        Some(r) => r.to_string(),\n        None => match detect_repo_from_cwd(&st) {\n            Some(r) => {\n                info!(\"Auto-detected repo: {r}\");\n                r\n            }\n            None => {\n                error!(\"could not detect repo from current directory\");\n                info!(\"Usage: dual create <branch> --repo <name>\");\n                info!(\"Or run from inside a repo that was added with `dual add`.\");\n                return 1;\n            }\n        },\n    };\n\n    // Find an existing workspace for this repo\n    let existing = st.workspaces_for_repo(&repo);\n    if existing.is_empty() {\n        error!(\"repo '{repo}' not found. Run `dual add` inside the repo first.\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Check if this branch already exists\n    if st.has_workspace(&repo, branch) {\n        error!(\"workspace {repo}/{branch} already exists\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    // Get URL from existing entry\n    let url = existing[0].url.clone();"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Launch a specific workspace: clone → container → shell RC → tmux → attach.\nfn cmd_launch(workspace: &str) -> i32 {\n    let st = match state::load() {\n        Ok(s) => s,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };\n\n    let entry = match st.resolve_workspace(workspace) {\n        Some(e) => e,\n        None => {\n            error!(\"unknown workspace '{workspace}'\");\n            info!(\"Configured workspaces:\");\n            for ws in st.all_workspaces() {\n                let id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n                info!(\"  {id}\");\n            }\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };",
  "new_string": "/// Launch a specific workspace: clone → container → shell RC → tmux → attach.\nfn cmd_launch(workspace_arg: Option<&str>) -> i32 {\n    let st = match state::load() {\n        Ok(s) => s,\n        Err(e) => {\n            error!(\"{e}\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    };\n\n    // Resolve workspace: use arg if provided, otherwise auto-detect from cwd\n    let workspace_id;\n    let entry = if let Some(ws) = workspace_arg {\n        match st.resolve_workspace(ws) {\n            Some(e) => {\n                workspace_id = ws.to_string();\n                e\n            }\n            None => {\n                error!(\"unknown workspace '{ws}'\");\n                info!(\"Configured workspaces:\");\n                for w in st.all_workspaces() {\n                    let id = config::workspace_id(&w.repo, &w.branch);\n                    info!(\"  {id}\");\n                }\n                return 1;\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        match detect_workspace(&st) {\n            Some(e) => {\n                workspace_id = config::workspace_id(&e.repo, &e.branch);\n                info!(\"Auto-detected workspace: {workspace_id}\");\n                st.resolve_workspace(&workspace_id).unwrap()\n            }\n            None => {\n                error!(\"could not detect workspace from current directory\");\n                info!(\"Usage: dual launch [workspace]\");\n                return 1;\n            }\n        }\n    };"
}
```

```json
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/main.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Detect which workspace the current directory belongs to.\nfn detect_workspace(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<state::WorkspaceEntry> {",
  "new_string": "/// Detect the repo name from the current working directory.\n///\n/// Matches the git remote URL of the cwd against known workspace URLs in state.\nfn detect_repo_from_cwd(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<String> {\n    let (_, url, _) = detect_git_repo().ok()?;\n\n    // Match against known workspace URLs\n    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {\n        if ws.url == url {\n            return Some(ws.repo.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Also try matching by git root path against workspace paths\n    let cwd = std::env::current_dir().ok()?;\n    let root = std::process::Command::new(\"git\")\n        .args([\"rev-parse\", \"--show-toplevel\"])\n        .output()\n        .ok()\n        .filter(|o| o.status.success())\n        .map(|o| PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string()))?;\n\n    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {\n        let ws_dir = st.workspace_dir(ws);\n        if ws_dir == root || ws_dir == cwd {\n            return Some(ws.repo.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    None\n}\n\n/// Detect which workspace the current directory belongs to.\nfn detect_workspace(st: &state::WorkspaceState) -> Option<state::WorkspaceEntry> {"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/jeevanpillay/Code/@jeevanpillaystudios/dual/src/tmux.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Attach to an existing tmux session.\npub fn attach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {\n    let status = Command::new(\"tmux\")\n        .args([\"attach-session\", \"-t\", session_name])\n        .status()\n        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;\n\n    if !status.success() {\n        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {\n            operation: \"attach-session\".to_string(),\n            session: session_name.to_string(),\n            stderr: format!(\"exit code: {}\", status.code().unwrap_or(-1)),\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Attach to an existing tmux session.\n///\n/// If already inside tmux (`$TMUX` is set), uses `switch-client` to avoid\n/// nested sessions. Otherwise uses `attach-session`.\npub fn attach(session_name: &str) -> Result<(), TmuxError> {\n    let (cmd, op) = if is_inside_tmux() {\n        ([\"switch-client\", \"-t\", session_name], \"switch-client\")\n    } else {\n        ([\"attach-session\", \"-t\", session_name], \"attach-session\")\n    };\n\n    let status = Command::new(\"tmux\")\n        .args(cmd)\n        .status()\n        .map_err(|e| TmuxError::NotFound(e.to_string()))?;\n\n    if !status.success() {\n        return Err(TmuxError::Failed {\n            operation: op.to_string(),\n            session: session_name.to_string(),\n            stderr: format!(\"exit code: {}\", status.code().unwrap_or(-1)),\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Check if we're already inside a tmux session.\npub fn is_inside_tmux() -> bool {\n    std::env::var(\"TMUX\").is_ok_and(|v| !v.is_empty())\n}"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "/// Print workspace status table.\nfn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState) {\n    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();\n    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {\n        let workspace_id = config::workspace_id(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n        let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n        let session_name = tmux::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n\n        let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {\n            // Explicit path — check if the path exists\n            ws.path\n                .as_ref()\n                .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(\".git\").exists())\n                .unwrap_or(false)\n        } else {\n            clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)\n        };\n        let container_st = container::status(&container_name);\n        let tmux_alive = tmux::is_alive(&session_name);\n\n        let status_icon = match (&container_st, tmux_alive) {\n            (container::ContainerStatus::Running, true) => \"\\u{25cf} attached\",\n            (container::ContainerStatus::Running, false) => \"\\u{25cf} running\",\n            (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, _) => \"\\u{25cb} stopped\",\n            (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) if clone_exists => \"\\u{25cb} stopped\",\n            (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) => \"\\u{25cc} lazy\",\n        };\n\n        info!(\"  {workspace_id:<30} {status_icon}\");\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Print workspace status grouped by repo.\nfn print_workspace_status(st: &state::WorkspaceState) {\n    let workspace_root = st.workspace_root();\n\n    // Collect unique repo names in order of first appearance\n    let mut repos: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    for ws in st.all_workspaces() {\n        if !repos.contains(&ws.repo) {\n            repos.push(ws.repo.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    for repo in &repos {\n        println!(\"{repo}\");\n        for ws in st.workspaces_for_repo(repo) {\n            let container_name = config::container_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n            let session_name = tmux::session_name(&ws.repo, &ws.branch);\n\n            let clone_exists = if ws.path.is_some() {\n                ws.path\n                    .as_ref()\n                    .map(|p| PathBuf::from(p).join(\".git\").exists())\n                    .unwrap_or(false)\n            } else {\n                clone::workspace_exists(&workspace_root, &ws.repo, &ws.branch)\n            };\n            let container_st = container::status(&container_name);\n            let tmux_alive = tmux::is_alive(&session_name);\n\n            let (icon, status_text) = match (&container_st, tmux_alive) {\n                (container::ContainerStatus::Running, true) => {\n                    (\"\\u{25cf}\", \"running  (container: up, tmux: attached)\")\n                }\n                (container::ContainerStatus::Running, false) => {\n                    (\"\\u{25cf}\", \"running  (container: up, tmux: none)\")\n                }\n                (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, true) => {\n                    (\"\\u{25cb}\", \"stopped  (container: stopped, tmux: background)\")\n                }\n                (container::ContainerStatus::Stopped, false) => {\n                    (\"\\u{25cb}\", \"stopped  (container: stopped, tmux: none)\")\n                }\n                (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) if clone_exists => {\n                    (\"\\u{25cb}\", \"stopped  (not launched)\")\n                }\n                (container::ContainerStatus::Missing, _) => {\n                    (\"\\u{25cc}\", \"lazy     (not cloned yet)\")\n                }\n            };\n\n            let branch_display = config::decode_branch(&config::encode_branch(&ws.branch));\n            println!(\"  {branch_display:<24} {icon} {status_text}\");\n        }\n        println!();\n    }\n}"
}
```

```
   879→
   880→/// Detect git repo info from the current directory.
   881→fn detect_git_repo() -> Result<(PathBuf, String, String), String> {
   882→    // Get repo root
   883→    let root_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   884→        .args(["rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"])
   885→        .output()
   886→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   887→    if !root_output.status.success() {
   888→        return Err("not inside a git repository".to_string());
   889→    }
   890→    let root = PathBuf::from(String::from_utf8_lossy(&root_output.stdout).trim());
   891→
   892→    // Get remote URL
   893→    let url_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   894→        .args(["remote", "get-url", "origin"])
   895→        .output()
   896→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   897→    let url = if url_output.status.success() {
   898→        String::from_utf8_lossy(&url_output.stdout)
   899→            .trim()
   900→            .to_string()
   901→    } else {
   902→        root.to_string_lossy().to_string() // local-only repo, use path as URL
   903→    };
   904→
   905→    // Get current branch
   906→    let branch_output = std::process::Command::new("git")
   907→        .args(["rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"])
   908→        .output()
   909→        .map_err(|_| "git not found".to_string())?;
   910→    let branch = String::from_utf8_lossy(&branch_output.stdout)
   911→        .trim()
   912→        .to_string();
   913→
   914→    Ok((root, url, branch))
   915→}
   916→
   917→/// Derive a short repo name from a directory path.
   918→/// "/Users/jeevan/code/lightfast" → "lightfast"
   919→fn derive_repo_name(path: &std::path::Path) -> String {
   920→    path.file_name()
   921→        .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
   922→        .unwrap_or("repo")
   923→        .to_string()
   924→}
```

```json
  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn tmux_availability_check_runs() {\n        // This test just verifies the function doesn't panic\n        // It may return true or false depending on the system\n        let _available = is_available();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn is_inside_tmux_detects_env() {\n        // Save and restore TMUX env var\n        let original = std::env::var(\"TMUX\").ok();\n\n        // When TMUX is set to a non-empty value, we're inside tmux\n        std::env::set_var(\"TMUX\", \"/tmp/tmux-1000/default,12345,0\");\n        assert!(is_inside_tmux());\n\n        // When TMUX is empty, we're not inside tmux\n        std::env::set_var(\"TMUX\", \"\");\n        assert!(!is_inside_tmux());\n\n        // When TMUX is unset, we're not inside tmux\n        std::env::remove_var(\"TMUX\");\n        assert!(!is_inside_tmux());\n\n        // Restore original value\n        match original {\n            Some(v) => std::env::set_var(\"TMUX\", v),\n            None => std::env::remove_var(\"TMUX\"),\n        }\n    }\n}"
}
```
