> AGENT

---

**Phase 2 Complete - Ready for Manual Verification**

All automated verification passed:
- **91 library tests** + **19 main tests** + **3 e2e tests** + **7 fixture tests** + **10 harness tests** = **130 tests passing**
- `cargo clippy` — clean
- `cargo fmt --check` — clean

### Summary of changes:

**1. Context-aware `dual create`** (`cli.rs`, `main.rs`)
- CLI changed from `dual create <repo> <branch>` to `dual create <branch> [--repo NAME]`
- When `--repo` is omitted, auto-detects by matching cwd's git remote URL against state

**2. Context-aware `launch`, `destroy`** (`cli.rs`, `main.rs`)
- `workspace` arg now optional for both commands
- When omitted, uses `detect_workspace()` to find current workspace from cwd

**3. Tmux nested session detection** (`tmux.rs`)
- `attach()` checks `$TMUX` env var
- If inside tmux: uses `switch-client -t` (no nesting)
- If outside tmux: uses `attach-session -t` (original behavior)
- Added `is_inside_tmux()` public function

**4. Improved `dual list` output** (`main.rs`)
- Workspaces now grouped by repo
- Shows branch name, status icon, and detailed container/tmux state
- Format: `repo` header → indented branches with status

Please perform the manual verification steps:
- [ ] `cd ~/code/myproject && dual create feat/auth` auto-detects repo
- [ ] `cd ~/code/myproject && dual launch` auto-detects workspace
- [ ] Running `dual launch` from inside tmux uses `switch-client` (no nesting)
- [ ] `dual list` shows grouped output by repo

Let me know when manual testing is complete so I can proceed to Phase 3 (scoping only — no implementation).

   443→
   444→## Phase 3: v3.0.0 — Architecture Rewrite (Scoping Only)
   445→
   446→### Overview
   447→This phase is scoped but NOT detailed for implementation yet. It defines what v3 contains so the v2.x work doesn't accidentally paint us into a corner.
   448→
   449→### 3.1 Multiplexer Trait
   450→
   451→Extract `src/tmux.rs` functions into a trait:
   452→```rust
   453→pub trait MultiplexerBackend {
   454→    fn create_session(&self, name: &str, cwd: &Path, init_cmd: Option<&str>) -> Result<()>;
   455→    fn attach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   456→    fn detach(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   457→    fn destroy(&self, name: &str) -> Result<()>;
   458→    fn is_alive(&self, name: &str) -> bool;
   459→    fn list_sessions(&self) -> Vec<String>;
   460→    fn send_keys(&self, name: &str, keys: &str) -> Result<()>;
   461→    fn session_name(&self, repo: &str, branch: &str) -> String;
   462→}
   463→```
   464→
   465→Implement `TmuxBackend`, stub `ZellijBackend`, add `BasicBackend` (no multiplexer).
   466→
   467→### 3.2 TUI
   468→
   469→Add ratatui + crossterm dependencies. New `src/tui/` module:
   470→- `mod.rs` — TUI app state and event loop
   471→- `tree.rs` — repo/branch tree view
   472→- `status.rs` — workspace status panel
   473→
   474→`dual` (no args) launches TUI. `dual list` becomes the non-interactive fallback.
   475→
   476→### 3.3 Session Layout Configuration
   477→
   478→Add to `.dual.toml`:
   479→```toml
   480→[layout]
   481→type = "editor-claude-shell"  # or custom
   482→
   483→[[layout.panes]]
   484→command = "nvim ."
   485→size = "60%"
   486→
   487→[[layout.panes]]
   488→command = "claude"
   489→size = "20%"
   490→
   491→[[layout.panes]]
   492→command = ""  # shell
   493→size = "20%"
   494→```
   495→
   496→### 3.4 Plugin System (Future)
   497→
   498→Design TBD. Likely TOML-declared hooks + shell commands:
   499→```toml
   500→[hooks]
   501→post_launch = "pnpm install && pnpm dev"
   502→pre_destroy = "pnpm build"
```

{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat: v2.1 + v2.2 — bug fixes, config wiring, context-aware CLI\n\nv2.1.0 — Bug Fixes & Completions:\n- Fix branch model: clone from local main workspace with --local + checkout -b\n- Fix default .dual.toml: hand-crafted commented template via write_default_hints()\n- Wire env vars to container creation (-e KEY=VALUE flags)\n- Wire setup command execution (docker exec sh -c after container start)\n- Configurable container commands via extra_commands in .dual.toml\n- Configurable anonymous volumes via anonymous_volumes in .dual.toml\n\nv2.2.0 — Context Awareness & UX:\n- Context-aware `dual create`: auto-detect repo from cwd (--repo now optional)\n- Context-aware `dual launch` and `dual destroy`: workspace arg now optional\n- Tmux nested session detection: uses switch-client when $TMUX is set\n- Improved `dual list`: grouped by repo with detailed status\n\nCloses DUAL-45, DUAL-46, DUAL-47, DUAL-48, DUAL-49, DUAL-50\nCloses DUAL-51, DUAL-52, DUAL-53, DUAL-54\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit all changes"
}

```
     1→# Changelog
     2→
     3→All notable changes to Dual are documented in this file.
     4→
     5→## [2.0.0] - 2026-02-15
     6→
     7→Complete rewrite from TypeScript to Rust. Dual is now a compiled binary with Docker-based workspace isolation, transparent command routing, and a reverse proxy for browser access.
     8→
     9→### Added
    10→
    11→- **Rust rewrite** — Full rewrite in Rust (Edition 2024) replacing the TypeScript/npm implementation
    12→- **Docker container isolation** — Each workspace runs in its own Docker container with bind-mounted source
    13→- **Transparent command routing** — Shell RC intercepts runtime commands (`pnpm`, `node`, `curl localhost`) and routes them to containers via `docker exec`
    14→- **Reverse proxy** — HTTP reverse proxy (hyper + tokio) with `{repo}-{branch}.localhost:{port}` subdomain routing for browser access
    15→- **tmux session management** — Automatic tmux sessions per workspace with attach/detach lifecycle
    16→- **Two-file config system** — Split architecture: `.dual.toml` (per-repo hints, committed to git) + `~/.dual/workspaces.toml` (global state, managed by Dual)
    17→- **`dual add` command** — Register an existing git repo as a Dual workspace
    18→- **`dual create` command** — Create a new branch workspace for an existing repo
    19→- **`dual sync` command** — Propagate shared config files (`.vercel`, `.env.local`, etc.) across branch workspaces using symlinks (Unix) or copies (Windows)
    20→- **`dual shell-rc` command** — Generate shell RC for transparent command routing (internal use)
    21→- **Shared config propagation** — Declare shared files in `.dual.toml` `[shared]` section, synced across all branches of a repo via `~/.dual/shared/{repo}/`
    22→- **Atomic state writes** — File locking (`fs2`) + temp file + atomic rename for crash-safe state persistence
    23→- **Structured logging** — `tracing` + `tracing-subscriber` with `DUAL_LOG` environment variable filter
    24→- **Typed errors** — `thiserror`-based error types replacing string errors
    25→- **curl-based installer** — Shell and PowerShell install scripts via cargo-dist
    26→- **Cross-platform builds** — Pre-built binaries for macOS (Apple Silicon + Intel), Linux (ARM64 + x64), and Windows (x64)
    27→- **CI/CD pipeline** — GitHub Actions with check/lint, unit tests, E2E tests (Docker + tmux), and automated releases
    28→
    29→### Changed
    30→
    31→- **Architecture** — Worktree-based workflows replaced with full git clone per workspace
    32→- **Config format** — Single `dual.toml` replaced with two-file system (`.dual.toml` + `workspaces.toml`)
    33→- **Installation** — npm package replaced with compiled binary (`cargo install` or curl installer)
    34→- **Language** — TypeScript → Rust (Edition 2024)
    35→
    36→### Removed
    37→
    38→- npm package distribution
    39→- Git worktree-based workspace management
    40→- dotenv multiplexer functionality
    41→- Environment variable file loading
    42→
    43→---
    44→
